# Round-3 review (2026-09-22 ~09:30 UTC) of the SECOND codex revision (121,774 bytes, sha 5dea1702): compact record

This file is the round-3 review page (status/claude-review-design-new-tensor-type.html at round 3, archived as -r3.html) reduced to its findings and recommendations.

## Short version of the round-3 page
- The second revision resolved 60 of the 87 open items (64 round-2 findings plus 23 round-1 residuals). 25 are partly resolved, 2 are deferred to jhan, none is unresolved and none regressed.
- This round found 52 new defects: 1 major, 27 minor, 24 notes. The major one is a single missing sentence: the design no longer says how detail::scaled_v_expr, the weighted V sum in kv_cache.hpp, obtains the packed address under the new access rule (G01). The other items an implementer would stop on are the t_amx_numerics fixture route (G22), the constraint mechanism (G09) and the D5 wording (G04).
- Verdict: accept with changes. All three judges say so; two of three say the design is not yet implementable until G01 and G22 are written. After those two edits and the eleven regressions and residuals listed in section 7, another agent can implement the parent from this page.

## Round-3 verdict (three judges)
- Implementing agent's view: accept with changes; implementable now. Reasons: I could start building the parent from this page today. The forward declarations, the owner, the view, the row expression, and the three typed operations (append_v_row, load_row, copy_token) have declared signatures and stated semantics [design-r3.txt:232-260]. The accessor table gives every existing caller a replacement [design-r3.txt:264-277], the two kernel signatures are complete [design-r3.txt:277], the arena construction names the array placement-new form, its two entry points, and the arena filter [design-r3.txt:338-352], and the step table and behavior-check table name files and evidence [design-r3.txt:371-378, :434-445]. Every source fact I rechecked matches live main 1400fa4481, which is identical to 2880c3aa9b for every file the parent touches [facts-r3.md:40-43]. I would stop once, at step 2. Line 249 restricts detail::v_vnni_access (the friend function that returns the packed pointer) to typed tensor operations and V-specific kernels and says cache callers do not use it [design-r3.txt:249]. Line 273 makes detail::scaled_v_expr, which lives in kv_cache.hpp, take the packed view [design-r3.txt:273], and line 329 says it obtains the physical address only inside the typed implementation [design-r3.txt:329]. Today that struct reads the address itself through reinterpret_cast<const bf16x32*>(data + i * (head_size / chunk_size)) [h/tron/models/kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2016-2018]. The page does not say whether scaled_v_expr may call the friend accessor or must move into v_vnni.hpp. Two implementers would resolve this differently and produce different PR shapes on the PV weighted-sum path, which runs on every decode step, so the design owner should choose (G01, major). A second, smaller gap: the PV case of t_amx_numerics hand-packs a std::vector<bf16> page and passes v_page.data() to weights_times_v_128x4 [t/t_amx_numerics.cpp@2880c3aa9b:203-227]; the typed signature rejects that pointer, and the page names no fixture route (fixture = a test's prepared input data) for this test, although the route is derivable from the fixture exception at line 317 and the owner's as_view() [design-r3.txt:317, :237-238] (G22, minor). Neither gap makes an implementer build the wrong thing, and either resolution of each merges, so the design is implementable now; the verdict is "accept with changes" only because G01 is a contradiction in the design text on that path. The 24 partly items and 2 deferred items are wording, citation, and evidence-labeling residuals; I confirmed the S18 label swap (the [Main generated save call] link points at the save_k_helper emission at ingest/src/TronCpp.hs@30c4ac82cb:2338 and the [Helper] link at the save_k emission at :2406) and the four LoopyTronSpec assertions that change (ingest/test/LoopyTronSpec.hs@30c4ac82cb:2801, :2812, :3225, :3231), but none of these changes what gets built. The 8-lane class-completeness rule is right as the code stands: the fake kernels at t/t_amx_dispatch_dtype.cpp@2880c3aa9b:180-190 sit outside the #ifdef TRON_AMX_DISPATCH at :192. Separately, 64 finding links and 2 page links point into claude-review-design-new-tensor-type.html [design-r3-links.tsv], the file this round's review overwrites, while the round-2 page is archived as -r2.html; repoint them (G03). The Q1-Q4 + D5 record is adequate for both readers: each row carries a proposed choice, the effect of rejection, and a status; the owner is named as jhan-positron; posting is gated on the owner's authorization; Q2 states and explains its departure from the sketch's span-like const rule; D5 attributes native-K typing to the issue author, not the reviewer [design-r3.txt:83-101]. One wording slip remains: D5 says "reuse the existing view alias", but const_native_k_view is new in this design (the underlying view type exists) [design-r3.txt:99, :232] (G04, minor).
  Top three edits: G01 (major). At design-r3.txt:273 and :329, and again in step 2 at :373, state one of: (A) detail::scaled_v_expr stays in kv_cache.hpp, its 16-lane constructor takes v_vnni_view<const bf16, page_size, head_size>, and it is a named permitted user of detail::v_vnni_access (amend :249 to list it); or (B) scaled_v_expr moves into h/tron/tensor/v_vnni.hpp as a typed operation and page::scaled_v forwards to it. In both cases keep the existing _mm512_load_si512 + _mm512_dpbf16_ps loop, its unroll order, and the (count + 1) / 2 * 2 bound [h/tron/models/kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2016-2036, :2066-2078]. | G22 + F35 (minor). Add to step 6 (design-r3.txt:377) or the 'AMX kernels and test substitutes' row (:276): 'The PV case of t/t_amx_numerics.cpp (:203-227 at 2880c3aa9b) cannot keep its hand-packed std::vector<bf16> page. Hold the page in a v_vnni_tensor<PAGE_TOKENS_64, HEAD_SIZE_128> (16,384 bytes, 64-byte aligned; static or heap storage), fill it with element writes through as_view() from the bf16_rne values (the fixture exception at :317), compute the reference from the same logical array, and pass owner.as_view() as the read-only view. The section-8 host-tensor check reads rows through row.bf16_chunk(i) or by assigning the packed matrix view to a bf16 tensor; it needs no hardware-tensor fixture.' | G09 (minor). In section 5 'Build and include boundaries' after 'Type constraints are positive even rows and positive columns divisible by chunk_size.' (design-r3.txt:283) add: 'Express every owner, view, and row constraint (even rows, columns divisible by chunk_size, T is bf16 or const bf16) as a static_assert inside the class body, not as a requires-clause on the template head. The forward declarations in kv_cache_fwd.hpp (:232) are unconstrained, and a redeclaration with a different requires-clause is a compile error.'
  Must fix before implementation: G01: record where detail::scaled_v_expr obtains the packed V address (keep it in kv_cache.hpp as a named user of detail::v_vnni_access, or move it into h/tron/tensor/v_vnni.hpp), at design-r3.txt:249, :273, :329 and :373. Until this sentence exists, two implementers would produce different PR shapes on the PV weighted-sum path.
- Maintainer's (Ben's) view: accept with changes; NOT yet implementable. Reasons: The design answers all three of my comments. Comment 1 (the worker index, r4065141577) is answered by merging save_k and save_k_helper into one save_k(..., worker_ix = 0, n_workers = 1) and deleting the helper entry points [design-r3.txt:420-424]; the child's two generated call sites exist where the design says (save_k_helper at ingest/src/TronCpp.hs@30c4ac82cb:2338, save_k at :2406). Comment 2 (review 5270587330) asked for an expr-based packed tensor with row-wise load and store operations; the design gives v_vnni_tensor, v_vnni_view and v_vnni_row that inherit expr, plus append_v_row, load_row and copy_token [design-r3.txt:238-240, 255], and it types both kernel arguments [design-r3.txt:277]. Comment 3 (my sketch, 5765866077) is followed on every point I would insist on: an aligned owner with lvalue-only as_view [design-r3.txt:238-239], private pointer construction with mutable-to-const conversion only [design-r3.txt:248], bounds-checked at() [design-r3.txt:245], the arena constructs the real storage type and never casts the old SIMD array [design-r3.txt:344-352], no clearing is added [design-r3.txt:118, 346], k_storage is selected by std::conditional_t [design-r3.txt:399], and the page accessors and the AVX-512 fallback take typed views [design-r3.txt:404, 407]. Where the design departs from the sketch it says so and asks: the address formula lives in the view (Q1, which matches my own stated doubt), const applies at both ranks (Q2), operator[] returns a non-contiguous logical row (Q3), and the first scope is smaller (Q4) [design-r3.txt:95-98]. Nothing is attributed to me that I did not write: the ids and times in the table match the saved JSON (18:24:52; 18:53:47; 18:58:31 edited 18:59:58) [design-r3.txt:67-69; evidence/github-review.json], "drafted his GitHub sketch with his own Claude session" paraphrases my "Claude and I came up with the following straw-man" [design-r3.txt:46], and every proposal is labelled "Proposed; reviewer answer not recorded" [design-r3.txt:95-99]. One phrase is stronger than what I wrote: "the reviewer's uniform-expression requirement" [design-r3.txt:412]; my review said "I think the way I would approach this". The one gap I would not let implementation start with is my own boundary rule applied to scaled_v_expr. The design says the internal accessor is for typed operations and V-specific kernels only and that cache callers do not use it [design-r3.txt:249]. It also says scaled_v_expr obtains the physical address only inside the typed implementation [design-r3.txt:329]. But scaled_v_expr is cache code in kv_cache.hpp that dereferences a raw bf16s* today [h/tron/models/kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:1990-1993, 2087-2088], and the design does not say which rule wins. My sketch allowed "SIMD and AMX implementations" to use the helper, so the design should say that scaled_v_expr is such an implementation, or it should move the body and list that move. The same silence covers the t_amx_numerics PV fixture, which passes a std::vector<bf16> pointer to weights_times_v_128x4 today [t/t_amx_numerics.cpp@2880c3aa9b:203-227] and has no permitted typed route under the design's rules (no public pointer construction, no test use of the helper). Smaller slips: D5 calls const_native_k_view "the existing view alias" [design-r3.txt:99], but the alias is new in kv_cache_fwd.hpp [design-r3.txt:232]; the type it names is the existing one (const_view<bf16,true,false,seq<R,C>> with the row_major default stride sseq<C,1>, h/tron/tensor/views.hpp@2880c3aa9b:31-36, kv_cache.hpp:2215-2219). The accessor names are not parallel (page::v_view versus page::k_packed [design-r3.txt:271, 404]) where my sketch used v_packed and k_packed. The headers diverge from my single h/tron/tensor/vnni.hpp into v_vnni.hpp and k_vnni.hpp [design-r3.txt:391]; round 2 judged that no change required (N59), and I accept it as the design's choice. The verdict is accept with changes: the interface and the decision record are what I asked for, and two short text edits settle the only questions an implementer could get wrong.

It is a V-specific SIMD implementation, which the sketch allows to use the helper.' - Risk reviewer's view: accept with changes; NOT yet implementable. Reasons: The design now states the numerics, lifetime and concurrency rules an implementer needs, and I checked the load-bearing ones against source. The NaN table is right: fp32 0x7F800001 truncates to bf16 0x7F80 (+infinity) in the packed append, and the generic conversion gives 0x7F81 (a NaN) only when TRON_IGNORE_NAN is absent [design-r3.txt:334; h/common/numerics/bf16.hpp@f46e48ba:92-109]. The existing padding test poisons the odd row through page::set_v with a float quiet NaN (0x7FC00000, which truncates to 0x7FC0 and stays a NaN), so the design's poison route survives the removal of v_data [design-r3.txt:436; t/t_llama_unit.cpp@2880c3aa9b:2776-2792]. Today's packed set_v already uses aligned source loads (_mm256_load_epi32 for bf16 and fp16 rows, _mm512_load_ps for float rows), so the design's 64-byte aligned-source rule preserves an existing precondition rather than adding a new crash risk [design-r3.txt:257; kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2300-2312, :2380]. The constructor sets storage_slot_count_ in its initializer list before it calls allocate_kv_group, so slot enumeration inside the construction helper sees the right slot count [design-r3.txt:348; kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:1091-1103]. A placement array-new probe on a trivially destructible 64-byte-aligned 32,768-byte block returned the same address as the arena at -O0 and -O2 with g++ 11.4 (clang 19, the pinned compiler, is not available on this host), so the construction form at design-r3.txt:344 is low risk. Pair-write serialization and one-lane-per-line K stores are stated [design-r3.txt:318-319, :410]. Two gaps stop a compiling first draft. detail::scaled_v_expr lives in kv_cache.hpp, reads the pair-tile base through reinterpret_cast of a raw bf16s pointer, and receives kv_block.v[0] from page::scaled_v [kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2016-2017, :2085-2086, :2260-2261]. The design forbids cache code from using detail::v_vnni_access, requires a packed view in that constructor, and names no route from the view to the tile base [design-r3.txt:249, :273, :329]. The AMX PV numerics case passes a hand-packed std::vector<bf16> to weights_times_v_128x4 [t/t_amx_numerics.cpp@2880c3aa9b:188-219]. The typed parameter has no pointer route, and the design names no typed route for this test [design-r3.txt:277, :377]. One CI risk needs a stronger done criterion. The kernel call sites in self_attention.hpp are inside #ifdef TRON_AMX_DISPATCH [self_attention.hpp@2880c3aa9b:1571, :1654, :1680]. The required Nix lane never sets that option [nix/cmake-tron-test-build.nix@2880c3aa9b:79-102]. The fakes at t/t_amx_dispatch_dtype.cpp@2880c3aa9b:47-190 compile in every build, so required CI catches a declaration mismatch but not a broken typed caller. The design asks for the AMX-on build [design-r3.txt:453], but the definition of done only says that build is reported separately [design-r3.txt:498]. The 24 partly and 2 deferred residuals are wording, citation and evidence-hygiene items. None makes the agent build the wrong thing. The G04 slip (D5 says reuse the existing view alias, but const_native_k_view is new in section 5 [design-r3.txt:99, :232]) is a wording fix, not a decision gap.
  Top three edits: G01. Settle the packed-address route for detail::scaled_v_expr. Add to the page::scaled_v row (design-r3.txt:273), repeat in step 2 (:373), and reconcile with :249 and :329, ONE of: (A) 'detail::scaled_v_expr stays in kv_cache.hpp and is a V-specific consumer: its 16-lane constructor takes v_vnni_view<const bf16, page_size, head_size>, obtains the pair-tile base once through detail::v_vnni_access (friend), and keeps the existing _mm512_load_si512 and _mm512_dpbf16_ps loops, unrolling and addition order (kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2016-2017, :2085-2086)'; or (B) 'move the pair-tile weighted-sum loop into h/tron/tensor/v_vnni.hpp as a typed operation taking the view, the scales, count and scratch; scaled_v_expr calls it and holds no address'. Then change :249 so the sentence "Cache callers and tests do not use these helpers" names scaled_v_expr as the one allowed cache-side user (option A) or stays as written (option B). Without this the implementer must break one of two rules to make the hot decode weighted sum compile. | G22. Name the typed route for the AMX PV numerics case. Add to step 6 (design-r3.txt:377) or the 'AMX kernels and test substitutes' row (:275): 'The PV case of t/t_amx_numerics.cpp (@2880c3aa9b:188-219) cannot keep passing its hand-packed std::vector<bf16> to weights_times_v_128x4. Hold the page in a v_vnni_tensor<PAGE_TOKENS_64, HEAD_SIZE_128> (16,384 bytes, 64-byte aligned; static or heap, not a stack array inside a Catch section if stack size is a concern), fill it with 64 append_v_row calls from the 64 bf16 rows (exact bits, so the bf16_rne values are unchanged), and pass owner.as_view(). Keep the hand-packed vector as the expected byte image and compare it to a memcpy of the trivially copyable owner, as :416 already does for K, so the test still checks the layout formula independently of the type.' | CI and definition of done. Add to design-r3.txt:493-499: 'Before the parent leaves draft, the 16-lane AMX-on native build (cmake --preset native -DAVX512=ON -DTRON_AMX_DISPATCH=ON) must compile and the fake dispatch cases of t_amx_dispatch_dtype must pass, with the configure log and Catch2 case lines recorded in the PR description. Reason: the typed kernel call sites in self_attention.hpp sit inside #ifdef TRON_AMX_DISPATCH (2880c3aa9b:1571, 1654, 1680) and the required Nix lane never sets that option (nix/cmake-tron-test-build.nix:79-102), so required CI compiles only the fakes (t/t_amx_dispatch_dtype.cpp:47-190) and cannot catch a broken typed caller.' Also add the G26 required-check list (README.ci.md@2880c3aa9b:76-79) and, in section 6 at :344, one runtime check: 'TRON_ASSERT that the pointer returned by the array new-expression equals region_base; this catches any array cookie the pinned compiler might add.'
  Must fix before implementation: G01: state in design-r3.txt:249, :273 and :329 how detail::scaled_v_expr obtains the packed pair-tile base (option A: friend access from cache code for this one consumer; option B: move the weighted-sum loop into v_vnni.hpp as a typed operation). Source: kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2016-2017, :2085-2086, :2260-2261. | G22: state in design-r3.txt:377 or :275 the typed route for the t_amx_numerics PV page (v_vnni_tensor<64,128> owner filled by append_v_row, view passed by value; hand-packed vector kept as expected bytes). Source: t/t_amx_numerics.cpp@2880c3aa9b:188-219.

## Round-3 section 7: recommended edits, in order
1. G01 (major). At design-r3.txt:273 and again in step 2 (:373) write: "detail::scaled_v_expr stays in kv_cache.hpp. Its 16-lane constructor takes v_vnni_view<const bf16, page_size, head_size>, obtains the pair-tile base once through detail::v_vnni_access, and keeps today's _mm512_load_si512 and _mm512_dpbf16_ps loop, unrolling and addition order (kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2066-2109)." At :249 change "Cache callers and tests do not use these helpers" to name scaled_v_expr (and weights_times_v_128x4) as the permitted V-specific users. If the weighted sum is meant to move into v_vnni.hpp instead, say so and list the move in steps 1 and 2.
2. G22 (minor, two judges' must-fix). Add to step 6 (:377) or the kernels row (:275): the PV case of t/t_amx_numerics.cpp (:198-227 at 2880c3aa9b) holds its page in a v_vnni_tensor<PAGE_TOKENS_64, HEAD_SIZE_128> owner (16,384 bytes, 64-byte aligned), fills it through the public typed operations (append_v_row from aligned bf16 rows, or element writes per the fixture rule at :317), passes owner.as_view(), and keeps its independent pair-formula check on bytes copied out of the owner, as :416 prescribes for t_k_vnni_layout. State whether a memcpy of hand-packed bytes into the owner is allowed in tests.
3. G09 (minor). After "Type constraints are positive even rows and positive columns divisible by chunk_size." (:283) add that every owner, view and row constraint is a static_assert inside the class body, never a requires-clause on the class template: kv_cache_fwd.hpp declares the templates unconstrained and cannot name chunk_size or TRON_CHUNK_SIZE (both live in the intrinsics header), and a redeclaration with different constraints does not compile. Repeat under the k_storage snippet (:399-401).
4. G04 (minor). D5 cell (:99): "Reuse the existing native-K view type (the type kv_block::k_view() returns) through the new const_native_k_view alias of section 5, and change the native-K kernel parameter." Name the alias where :267 and :518 say "The alias".
5. The issue-text block (G06, G07, G05; closes the N42 residual and prepares F19-F21 for jhan). At :107 write that the draft adds a problem paragraph, a terms list and one Scope bullet, replaces no existing Scope bullet and no PR-order item, and replaces the two occurrences of the reviewer's name with "the reviewing maintainer's" with the three links unchanged. Replace "child-adaptation checklist" at :107 and :120 with "PR order item 2" (item 3 is a placement rule). Give the role sentence at :118 its two review links, and add the 8-lane qualification to the packed-V term (F19).
6. G19 and M04 (minor). Heterogeneous bullet (:350): the helper calls the lambda once per active logical slot index with that slot's compile-time geometry; form the physical offset as kv_storage_offset(slot), as fill_random does; delete the logical-alias sentence.
7. Header homes (G17, G16, N58). At :391 name h/tron/tensor/k_vnni.hpp as the home of the typed K bulk operations (store_row, load_row, store_block, token copy, plane copy) and of detail::k_vnni_access, with the three detail helpers moving with them and h/tron/kernels/k_vnni.hpp keeping only qk_group; add the K forward declarations to kv_cache_fwd.hpp so amx_attn_iface.hpp can declare qk_vnni_128x4 by value without including an intrinsics header; write the paths in full at :249 and :284.
8. Regressions of the round-3 edit, one line each: restore "Adding -DTRON_AMX_DISPATCH=ON to cmake_args in nix/cmake-tron-test-build.nix (lines 79-103)" at :465 (G29, F16); restore the two kernel-call rejections with function names in the typed-boundary row at :438 (G30, M03); swap the two S18 generated-save-call links (G34, N33, F29); move [Host guard] to the campaign_guard_acquire bullet and cite exec/bill-share.sh for the marker and instance halves at :481 (G28); scope the reinterpret_cast search to kv_cache.hpp and v_vnni.hpp and say what it checks (G21); replace "poison-fill" with "random finite-value fill" for book::fill_random at :274 and :441 (G15); spell the forward declarations with the struct key and point at expr.hpp:17-22 (G08); fix the three line ranges 2055-2065, 224-229, CMakeLists.txt#L65-L66 (G18).
9. Process items: add make lint-notes to the local gate list and name the Note convention for the relocated documentation (H01); name ingest/AGENTS.md and make format-haskell in the child port (H02); carry and refresh the child's three test-cost rows under the section 8 authorization rule (G24); say where the Slice case logs live, that the next checkout deletes them, and what to record when the runner is unreachable (G25); add the cost-record refresh to the definition of done and, per the risk judge, require the 16-lane AMX-on build to compile and the fake dispatch cases to pass before the parent leaves draft (G26); name the t_amx_numerics log without an "AMX unavailable" WARN line plus a TRON_AMX_DISABLE=1 control run as the kernel-ran evidence (G49).
10. The remaining partly items of section 3, each one sentence at the cited line: M01, N56 (with G37's inside/outside list), F29, N11, M06, N44, F23, F57, F58, F54, F35, N20 (with G40), N50, F38 (with G31), N30, C01, N28 and C06 (same edit), N33, N59 (or keep the rename and say so), N42. Then repoint the 64 round-2 id links and the two page links to claude-review-design-new-tensor-type-r2.html and -r2-votes.html (G03), and refresh section 9 so every row's remaining-work cell names its open residual.

## Round-3 section 5: the decision record (question | covers | assessment)
- Q1. May each concrete view contain its address formula? Proposed: yes; one view per layout, separate V and K owners, no layout-policy template. | Ben's sketch sections 1 and 2 (layout as a template argument of owner and view) and his own reservation: "I'm not entirely convinced of the necessity of separate k_vnni_layout from vnni_view". | Adequate. The proposed choice follows Ben's stated doubt, and the fallback is the sketch itself, so either answer is implementable. The design also confirms jhan's reading of that reservation (design-r3.txt:71); N11 asks it to state what the reading was.
- Q2. Should const matrix and row objects prevent element mutation? Proposed: yes at both ranks, following tron's rank-2 view rule. | Ben's sketch: "As with std::span, a const view object does not make its elements const. vnni_view<const bf16, Layout> does." | Adequate, and the question most likely to be answered no, because Ben stated the opposite rule in writing. The design flags the departure and gives a fallback. Keep the const contracts in one table row so a flip is one edit, and settle the row-conversion clause first (N20, G40).
- Q3. May packed views expose logical rows through operator[]? Proposed: yes, returning a logical row expression with no public pointer; keep bounds-checked at(). | Ben's review asked for the expr precedent ("a uniform interface based upon expr"); his sketch excluded operator[] because it suggests a contiguous row. | Adequate. The design resolves the tension between the two comments in favor of expression reads with no pointer route, and names the fallback (remove the row expressions from both families). G12 notes that the mandated bf16_chunk override serves a test-only path.
- Q4. Is the smaller parent scope sufficient? Proposed: yes; expression reads and the existing typed bulk operations; slices, iterators and generic writes deferred. | Ben's review: "the usual operations that we expect of tensor types and, in particular, the row-wise load/store operations". | Adequate. The deferrals are explicit and reasoned. G39 asks to restore the one sentence that mapped append_v_row to the sketch's store_row, so Ben sees the name difference explained; the maintainer judge also asks for a sketch-name to design-name table.
- D5. Who owns native-K typing? Requested scope: the issue author; reuse the existing K view type through a new alias and change the native-K kernel parameter. | jhan's request ("introduces the new abstraction on VNNI-V and native K") and the issue title; not part of Ben's sketch. | Right attribution, one wording slip: the cell says "reuse the existing view alias", but const_native_k_view is new in section 5; the view type is what exists (G04). The record is copied into the PR description, so fix the cell before implementation.
- What the record still lacks | jhan's step-4 premise (the child's packed-K change becomes a local change within the new type) and the identity of "the issue author", "the assignee" and "the user". | Two notes: the premise is answered nowhere, and it holds only in part (G37, N56 residual); the three role labels for one person are never tied together (G35). Neither blocks implementation.

## Round-3 section 6: what the second revision gets right
- design-r3.txt:148 labels 2880c3aa9b as saved review evidence and orders a fresh fetch before branching. This matches the live state: main moved to 1400fa4481 with no change to any parent step file (facts-r3.md:38-43).
- design-r3.txt:232-234 gives const_native_k_view explicit strides. That alias is the exact type of the const kv_block::k_view(): row_major<seq<R,C>> is sseq<C,1> (seq.hpp@2880c3aa9b:388-394), and const_view(view const&) at view.hpp@2880c3aa9b:299 converts the writable page::k result.
- design-r3.txt:267 names pg.template k<geometry.kv>(slot, kv_head) as the whole-plane replacement for k0.data. That two-argument overload exists (kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:1559-1569) and the caller it replaces is self_attention.hpp@2880c3aa9b:1652-1655.
- design-r3.txt:257 says the aligned flag preserves the existing aligned load/store contract. That is right: set_v loads the source with _mm256_load_epi32 and _mm512_load_ps (kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2300, 2308, 2339, 2380) and get_v stores with _mm512_store_ps and _mm256_store_si256 (2421, 2455). Every existing test buffer except heterogeneous_scheduler_compile.cpp:441 is alignas(64) or a tensor.
- design-r3.txt:344-347 builds the arena on placement array-new of a trivially default-constructible block. That holds: bf16s is __m256i (simd/types.hpp@2880c3aa9b:26, auto.hpp:54), and kv_block holds only k, v and member functions (kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2191-2241).
- design-r3.txt:362-363 lists ten comment sites for the V layout. A grep for 'interleav' over h, src and t at 2880c3aa9b finds no V-layout comment outside those ten sites and the set_v and append_v bodies that move with their code.
- design-r3.txt:452 says the 16-lane AMX-off row matches the required Nix feature combination. Verified: nix/cmake-tron-test-build.nix@2880c3aa9b:79-88 passes -DBUILD_NATIVE=OFF and -DAVX512=ON and no TRON_AMX_DISPATCH.
- design-r3.txt:454 and :457 keep t_amx_dispatch_dtype as the 8-lane target and mark the rest 'Insufficient data'. The target is sound: exercise_page is a function template (t/t_amx_dispatch_dtype.cpp@2880c3aa9b:62-63) and both TEST_CASEs sit under #ifdef TRON_AMX_DISPATCH (:192-214), so its unguarded set_v at :98 is never instantiated at 8 lanes.
- design-r3.txt:477 calls make iwyu-check optional. That holds beyond the SKILL.md rule: the lint recipe (GNUmakefile@2880c3aa9b:693-731), the Nix lint check body and gcp-nix.yml contain no IWYU step.
- design-r3.txt:424 names the direct three-thread calls in t/t_llama_unit.cpp. The child section at t/t_llama_unit.cpp@30c4ac82cb:2773-2782 calls save_k_helper for workers 1 and 2 and save_k(..., N_WORKERS_3) for worker 0, which the 'old positional calls' sentence catches.
- design-r3.txt:486 names gen/t_llama_unit. CMakeLists.txt@2880c3aa9b:66 sets CMAKE_RUNTIME_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY to the binary directory and the native preset's binaryDir is gen (CMakePresets.json@2880c3aa9b:34).
- design-r3.txt:148 says 98bb8cb2 is 79 commits after f46e48ba. git rev-list --count f46e48bab498..98bb8cb22f94 returns 79.
- design-r3.txt:583-584 S19 and S20 anchors point at the right code: test_runtime.py:30-51 (test_identity, passed_sentinel), slice_bench_manifest.py:14 (STANDARD_BENCH_WIDTHS = (1, 2, 4)), bin/slice:1996-2000 (capacity check), :1731-1736 (put_platform_bench), :3676-3684 (whole-box bench), .github/AGENTS.md:19-20.
- design-r3.txt:269 says the v_head_fn callback wraps its aligned scratch. src/tron/gof.cpp@2880c3aa9b:203 declares alignas(64) std::array<bf16, ...> v_scratch and :213 passes per-head offsets of HW_BAKED_HEAD_SIZE elements, so the destination view's alignment precondition holds.
- Section 9 lists every one of the 64 round-2 ids and the 58 retained round-1 ids exactly once, and its rejected-candidate sentence names the right 10 plus 6 ids (G52, a positive check by the section-9 auditor).
- The NaN bit table is right under all three conversion paths; the lead recomputed 0x7F800001 to 0x7F80, 0x7F81 and 0x7F80.

## Round-3 section 4: the 52 NEW findings (G.. finders, H.. completeness critic), with verifier corrections and lead notes

### G01 [round-3 NEW finding, severity MAJOR] scaled_v_expr is not named as a permitted user of detail::v_vnni_access, and its file home was dropped
- Where in the round-3 design: 5. Interface design, Internal storage access row (design-r3.txt:249) and page::scaled_v row (:273); 6. Conversions and weighted sums (:329); 7. step 2 (:373); 8. Definition of done (:496)
- Relates to earlier id(s): M07 N23 N24 N52
- Kind: ambiguity | found by lenses: handoff, cpp-interface
- Claim: detail::scaled_v_expr is defined inside kv_cache.hpp, which is cache code. Its 16-lane constructor loads whole 64-byte pair tiles with _mm512_load_si512 and feeds them to _mm512_dpbf16_ps. The design gives that constructor a v_vnni_view<const bf16, page_size, head_size>, and the view has no public pointer route (design-r3.txt:248). Line 249 allows detail::v_vnni_access only inside typed tensor operations and V-specific kernels, and says cache callers do not use it. Line 329 says 'Obtain the physical address only inside the typed implementation' without naming a mechanism. The round-3 edit deleted the round-2 sentence that kept scaled_v_expr in kv_cache.hpp. Reading through row.chunk would double the loads and change the instruction stream that line 329 says to preserve. An implementer cannot tell whether (a) scaled_v_expr counts as a V-specific kernel and may call the friend from kv_cache.hpp, or (b) the weighted-sum body must move into h/tron/tensor/v_vnni.hpp as a typed operation that section 5 does not list. The definition-of-done sentence at :496 cannot be checked until that decision is written.
- The round-3 design text said: "Use detail::v_vnni_access , a friend declared with the V types, only inside typed tensor operations and V-specific kernels. The child uses a separate detail::k_vnni_access in its own header. Cache callers and tests do not use these helpers. [...] Obtain the physical address only inside the typed implementation."
- Evidence given in round-3: design-r3.txt:249, :273 ('Use a packed read-only V view in the 16-lane constructor'), :329, :373 (step 2 no longer says where scaled_v_expr lives), :496, :39 (Words: 'A kernel performs a specific computation', which does not settle the question). Round-2 text evidence/claude-review-r2/design-revised-text.txt:223 ('detail::vnni_access is the internal friend used only by tensor operations and layout-specific kernels') and :353 ('Keep typed scaled_v_expr here unless a real include dependency requires a separate header'); design-wdiff.txt segments @old 2627-2638 (sentence added) and @old 4447 (placement sentence deleted). Source: h/tron/models/kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:1988-1993 (namespace detail, struct scaled_v_expr, member const bf16s* const data), :2066-2109 (constructor: const bf16x32* tile = reinterpret_cast<const bf16x32*>(data + i * (head_size / chunk_size)); _mm512_dpbf16_ps(t0, _mm512_load_si512(tile + 0), scale); eight tiles per pair for head_size 128), :2000-2007 (even_part/odd_part are private helpers, not the hot-loop read), :2252-2262 (page::scaled_v passes kv_block<geometry>(slot, kv_head).v[0]).
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): Add to the page::scaled_v row (design-r3.txt:273) and repeat in step 2 (:373) ONE of: (A) 'detail::scaled_v_expr stays in kv_cache.hpp. Its 16-lane constructor takes v_vnni_view<const bf16, page_size, head_size>, obtains the pair-tile base once through detail::v_vnni_access, and keeps today's _mm512_load_si512 and _mm512_dpbf16_ps loop, unrolled tiles, and addition order. Under the helper rule it is a V-specific kernel and the only kv_cache.hpp user of the helper; page, book, and tests do not call it.' or (B) 'Move the 16-lane weighted-sum body into h/tron/tensor/v_vnni.hpp as a typed operation weighted_sum(v_vnni_view<const bf16, R, D>, const tensor<R>& scales, size_t count, tensor<D>& scratch); scaled_v_expr's constructor calls it and stores no pointer.' Then change line 249 to name that user explicitly. If neither friend use nor a move is wanted, add to the view contract 'bf16x32 pair_tile(size_t pair, size_t tile) const noexcept' (the 64-byte tile of pair p at dimensions 16*tile .. 16*tile+15, both tokens interleaved; used only by scaled_v_expr and the PV kernel).
- LEAD NOTE (final grade major): All three verifiers hold the finding and narrow it: the two rules do not contradict each other, because line 249 forbids the helper to "cache callers" and the design never says whether detail::scaled_v_expr is one. The remaining text points to option A: step 2 keeps the typed weighted-sum inputs under kv_cache.hpp [design-r3.txt:373], the v_vnni.hpp operation list has no weighted sum [:253-259, :372], and section 9 says "Type both weighted-sum constructors" [:520]. The round-2 sentence that kept scaled_v_expr in kv_cache.hpp was deleted without any finding asking for it [design-wdiff.txt @old 4447]. The verifiers graded this minor because the reading is recoverable. I keep major: all three judges name it as the one sentence that must exist before implementation starts, and the PV weighted sum runs on every decode step, so two implementers must not resolve it differently. The fix is one sentence at :273 and :373 plus naming scaled_v_expr at :249.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (source lens): Narrow the title and kind. Replace "two access rules conflict" with "the helper rule at :249 does not say whether scaled_v_expr counts as a V-specific kernel, and :329 names no mechanism"; the rules conflict only under the reading that scaled_v_expr is a "cache caller". Change kind from contradiction to ambiguity. Fix one citation: namespace detail opens at kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:1987, so cite :1987-1993 (not :1988-1993). Add the provenance fact: no round-2 finding asked to delete the placement sentence, so its removal (design-wdiff.txt @old 4447) is an unrequested loss of specificity relative to the round-2 text (evidence/claude-review-r2/design-revised-text.txt:353). Keep the recommendation. Option A is the one consistent with step 2 (:373 keeps typed weighted-sum inputs in kv_cache.hpp) and with the deleted round-2 sentence, so present it as the default: add to :273 and :373 "detail::scaled_v_expr stays in kv_cache.hpp; its 16-lane constructor takes v_vnni_view<const bf16, page_size, head_size>, obtains the pair-tile base once through detail::v_vnni_access, and keeps today's _mm512_load_si512 / _mm512_dpbf16_ps loops (kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2078-2119)", and amend :249 to name scaled_v_expr as the one kv_cache.hpp user of the helper. Drop the sentence "The definition-of-done sentence at :496 cannot be checked until that decision is written"; it can be checked once the implementer records which reading was used, so say instead "the DoD at :496 needs the classification of scaled_v_expr written down to be checkable without a judgment call."
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (design-text lens): Narrow the title to "scaled_v_expr is not named as a permitted user of detail::v_vnni_access". Drop "two access rules conflict": the rules at design-r3.txt:249 and :329 conflict only if scaled_v_expr is a cache caller, and the design leaves that classification unstated rather than contradicting itself. Drop option (b) from the claim: the design already places the weighted sum in kv_cache.hpp through step 2 ("typed weighted-sum inputs" in the kv_cache.hpp row, design-r3.txt:373), step 1 (v_vnni.hpp contents list no weighted sum, :372) and section 5 (only append_v_row, load_row, copy_token move, :255-259). Corrected claim: "detail::scaled_v_expr stays in kv_cache.hpp (implied by step 2, design-r3.txt:373) and takes a packed read-only V view (design-r3.txt:273). Its 16-lane body loads whole 64-byte pair tiles [kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2066-2109], which needs a tile address the view does not expose publicly (design-r3.txt:248). The helper rule at design-r3.txt:249 allows detail::v_vnni_access only in typed tensor operations and V-specific kernels and says cache callers do not use it, and line 329 does not name the helper. The design never says scaled_v_expr counts as a V-specific kernel. The round-3 edit also deleted the explicit placement sentence (design-wdiff.txt @old 4447), so placement is now implied rather than stated." Corrected recommendation, one sentence, added to the page::scaled_v row (design-r3.txt:273) and referenced from the helper row (:249): "detail::scaled_v_expr stays in kv_cache.hpp, counts as a V-specific kernel under the helper rule, and is the only kv_cache.hpp user of detail::v_vnni_access: its 16-lane constructor obtains the pair-tile base once through the helper and keeps today's _mm512_load_si512 and _mm512_dpbf16_ps loop, unrolled tiles and addition order; page, book and tests do not call the helper." Optionally restore the deleted step-2 placement sentence. Severity minor. Relates to N52 and N24 (whose round-2 corrections already read "the typed implementation" at r2:316 as scaled_v_expr's own body) and M07 (helper per layout family, resolved).
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (intent lens): Narrow the title to "scaled_v_expr is not named as a permitted user of detail::v_vnni_access, and its file home was dropped". Delete the sub-claims "two access rules conflict" and "the definition-of-done sentence at :496 cannot be checked". Replace "An implementer cannot tell whether (a) or (b)" with "The remaining text points to (a): step 2 places typed weighted-sum inputs under kv_cache.hpp [design-r3.txt:373], section 9 says 'Type both weighted-sum constructors' [:520], and the v_vnni.hpp operation list has no weighted sum [:253-259, :371]; the design should say so in one sentence." Keep recommendation (A) only: add to the page::scaled_v row [design-r3.txt:273] "detail::scaled_v_expr stays in kv_cache.hpp. Its 16-lane constructor obtains the pair-tile base once through detail::v_vnni_access and keeps today's load and dot-product loop. Under the access rule it is a V-specific kernel." and add to line 249 "scaled_v_expr is the one kv_cache.hpp user." Drop option (B), which adds an operation the design deliberately does not list. Drop the pair_tile alternative: a public method that takes pair and tile indices exposes physical indexing through the view, which line 496 confines to tensor operations and kernels. Cite Ben's sketch lines 152 and 209 [github-review.json comments[3]] as the precedent for kernel-side pointer extraction. Severity minor.

### G02 [round-3 NEW finding, severity MINOR] Words table lacks eight terms the text uses; 'Valid token row' is defined but never used
- Where in the round-3 design: 1. Words used here (design-r3.txt:23-56); uses at :274, :300, :392, :407, :424, :438, :443, :452, :469, :470

It also lacks 'poison' (7 uses, first at :274), 'Catch2' and 'STATIC_REQUIRE / STATIC_REQUIRE_FALSE' (:469, :438), 'RUN_TESTS' (:470), 'GCP' (:469), 'qk_group' (:392, :407), and 'run()' and 'run_main_help' (:424). - The round-3 design text said: "| Valid token row | A row whose cache values are available to the current reader. |"
- Evidence given in round-3: design-r3.txt:23-56 (no row contains any of the eight terms); grep counts in design-r3.txt: poison 7 (first 274), TRON_AMX_DISPATCH 5 (first 452), TRON_AMX_DISABLE 1 (443), Catch2 1 (469), STATIC_REQUIRE 1 (438), RUN_TESTS 1 (470), GCP 1 (469), qk_group 2 (392, 407), run_main_help 1 (424), 'Valid token row' 1 (56 only). Round-2 text (evidence/claude-review-r2/design-revised-text.txt) counts: Catch2 0, STATIC_REQUIRE 0, RUN_TESTS 0, GCP 0, run_main_help 0, qk_group 1. Sources: CMakeLists.txt@2880c3aa9b:48 option(TRON_AMX_DISPATCH ... OFF); amx_attn_iface.hpp@30c4ac82cb:47 and amx_attn.cpp@30c4ac82cb:62 (TRON_AMX_DISABLE); .github/workflows/gcp-nix.yml@2880c3aa9b:538 RUN_TESTS; t/t_llama_unit.cpp@2880c3aa9b: 13 lines match 'poison', h/tron/models/kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b: 0; k_vnni.hpp@30c4ac82cb:74 (qk_group).
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): Add to the 'Build constants' row: 'TRON_AMX_DISPATCH is the CMake option that compiles the AMX attention kernels (default OFF). TRON_AMX_DISABLE=1 is a runtime environment variable that makes an AMX build take the non-AMX path.' Add a row 'Poison, Catch2, STATIC_REQUIRE': poison is a test fill of NaN bit patterns written on purpose so a read of an unwritten slot is detected; Catch2 is the C++ test framework of the t/ suites; STATIC_REQUIRE and STATIC_REQUIRE_FALSE are its compile-time assertion macros. Add a row 'RUN_TESTS, GCP': RUN_TESTS is the gcp-nix.yml variable that decides whether the Test host job executes tests; GCP is Google Cloud Platform, where the required Nix jobs run. Add to 'VNNI K and block save': qk_group is the child's AVX-512 score reader in h/tron/kernels/k_vnni.hpp; run() and run_main_help are the generated per-batch entry functions of the main thread and a helper thread. Delete the 'Valid token row' row or replace it with 'Live token | A token position whose cache values are complete; the live count is the number of such positions in a page.'
- LEAD NOTE (final grade minor): Holds 3 of 3. Corrections applied to the recommendation: poison has two meanings on the page (NaN in the padding tests; random values in book::fill_random at :274), so the Words row must say so; RUN_TESTS gates both the Test host and the Test FPGA jobs (gcp-nix.yml:538, :605); "GCP Nix" is the name of the required workflow, so define that, not the cloud provider; the TRON_AMX_DISPATCH row was already an optional item of round-1 F23, so that part is an F23 residual. Nine names in eight groups.

(CONTEXT.md:31-32), so rename the row to 'Live token, live count' and reuse that wording; the row was already present in the round-2 text (design-revised-text.txt:45). (g) Set relates_to to N06 and F23: N06 asked for code names to be defined at first use and this revision adds six new undefined names; F23 was the round-1 Words-table gap finding whose section-9 row (design-r3.txt:548) claims 'Expand definitions'.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (intent lens): Keep the finding at minor with these narrowings. (1) 'poison' has two referents in the design, and the Words row must say so: at :298-300 and :436 it means the NaN fill of a V padding row (t/t_llama_unit.cpp@2880c3aa9b:1919-1925 fills quiet_NaN through set_v and v().set; :2670 'append_v preserves poison'); at :274 'Keep its poison-fill behavior' it is attached to book::fill_random, which writes std::normal_distribution random values and, per its own comment, 'bypasses the zero-init V slot invariant' (kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:1055-1065); its test callers (t_llama_unit.cpp@2880c3aa9b:784-785, :2008 'Initialize a few pages of KV cache with random bf16 values') never say poison. Replace the recommended row text with: 'Poison: a deliberate fill with values that must never be read as valid data. The V padding tests use NaN. book::fill_random uses random values that break the zero-initialized V slot rule.' Or reword :274 to 'random-fill behavior'. (2) TRON_AMX_DISPATCH is the lowest-priority item: the table at :452-454 pairs 'AMX code off/on' with -DTRON_AMX_DISPATCH=OFF/ON, so the reader is not misled; the Words row can quote the option text 'Compile the AMX software-attention kernels and their dispatch' (CMakeLists.txt@2880c3aa9b:48). (3) 'GCP' at :469 is part of the workflow name: gcp-nix.yml@2880c3aa9b:1 is `name: GCP Nix`, and README.ci.md@2880c3aa9b:7, :63 call it 'GCP Nix'. Define 'GCP Nix' as the required workflow (.github/workflows/gcp-nix.yml) rather than only expanding the acronym; the repo files checked do not spell out 'Google Cloud Platform' (README.ci.md:10 says only 'GCP Filestore'), so state that expansion as an assumption or omit it. (4) RUN_TESTS gates the test steps of both the 'Test host' job (gcp-nix.yml@2880c3aa9b:538, :571-573) and the 'Test FPGA' job (:605); the row should say 'the test jobs', not only 'the Test host job'. (5) Drop 'per-batch' from the run()/run_main_help definition: TronCpp.hs@30c4ac82cb:25-28 says state::run is the generated main-thread method, which also launches the helper and attention workers, and state::run_main_help is the method each helper thread runs; both take the q_batches list (:1217). (6) qk_group: add 'child-only; it does not exist on main' (0 matches at 2880c3aa9b; the child's kv_cache.hpp@30c4ac82cb:1684 calls it 'the AVX-512 reader k_vnni::qk_group'). (7) The 'Valid token row' orphan is carried over, not new: the round-2 text also had it only at its line 45 while the body used 'live-token' and 'live count' (design-revised-text.txt:257, :283, :306, :316, :395, :463), and round-2 N24's intent-lens correction already recorded the define-at-first-use gap for 'live-token count' (r2-findings.md:372). Round 3 changed :273 to 'live count from page::count()', which ties the term to its source, but :298 and :320 still say 'live-token' with no definition. The recommended replacement row ('Live token | A token position whose cache values are complete; the live count is page::count(), the number of such positions in a page') is right; cite kv_cache.hpp@f46e48bab498:1483-1494 ('How many kv entries are live') as the source of the word. (8) Term count: the list has nine names if run() and run_main_help are counted separately; six are new in this revision (Catch2, STATIC_REQUIRE, RUN_TESTS, GCP, run(), run_main_help: all 0 in the round-2 text), and poison, qk_group, TRON_AMX_DISPATCH and TRON_AMX_DISABLE were already present in round 2 (3, 1, 5 and 1 lines).

### G04 [round-3 NEW finding, severity MINOR] D5 says 'reuse the existing view alias'; the alias is new in this design
- Where in the round-3 design: 2. Decision record, row D5 (design-r3.txt:99)
- Relates to earlier id(s): N14 N20 N22 N50
- Kind: ambiguity | found by lenses: regression-diff

Section 5 introduces both aliases in the new header h/tron/tensor/kv_cache_fwd.hpp (design-r3.txt:232). What exists is the view type itself, view<bf16, true, false, seq<page_size, head_size>>, which kv_block::k_view() already returns. Section 2's earlier bullet says this correctly ('the existing read-only matrix view', :76). Row D5 contradicts it and could send the implementer looking for an alias in the repository. Row D5 is a new round-3 segment.

- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): Change the D5 proposed-choice cell to: 'The issue author requested it. Reuse the existing native-K view type through the new const_native_k_view alias of section 5, and change the native-K kernel parameter.'
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (source lens): Narrow one detail: the k_view() citation range is 2207-2219 if the TRON(nodiscard, inline, pure) attribute lines are included, 2208-2219 for the function heads; the row_major machinery spans seq.hpp:377-394 (seq_stride at 377-379, row_major_ specialization at 387-390, alias at 393-394), not only 388-394. The recommendation stands as written; optionally add a section-5 reference in the cell so the implementer finds the alias definition: "The issue author requested it. Reuse the existing native-K view type (the type kv_block::k_view() returns today) through the new const_native_k_view alias declared in section 5, and change the native-K kernel parameter." [design-r3.txt:99, :232, :277; kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2207-2219]
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (design-text lens): Narrow the claim. The D5 cell alone reads as if an alias already existed; the body does not. Section 5 declares native_k_view and const_native_k_view inside the new header kv_cache_fwd.hpp [design-r3.txt:232-233], and section 7 uses "The alias" for that new alias [design-r3.txt:267]. So no wrong build results; the defect is inconsistent wording between D5 [design-r3.txt:99] and the section-2 bullet [design-r3.txt:76], in a row the design says to copy into the parent PR's Decisions record [design-r3.txt:91]. Keep the proposed cell text, or the shorter form: "The issue author requested it. Reuse the existing native-K view type (the type kv_block::k_view() returns) through the new const_native_k_view alias of section 5, and change the native-K kernel parameter." Citations for the evidence: h/tron/models/kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2208-2219 (k_view return types), h/tron/tensor/seq.hpp@2880c3aa9b:388-394 (row_major<seq<R,C>> = sseq<C,1>, so the explicit-stride alias names the same type), git grep native_k_view at 2880c3aa9b / 30c4ac82cb / 1400fa4481: no match.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (intent lens): Narrow "Row D5 contradicts it" to "Row D5 is inconsistent with the wording at design-r3.txt:76": the type is existing, the alias is new, so this is a naming slip, not a substantive contradiction. Add the likely cause: the aliases have been in the design since round 1 (round-1 F43 and round-2 N14 discuss them; the round-2 design text declares them at evidence/claude-review-r2/design-revised-text.txt:203 and :205), so "existing" probably meant "already declared in this design". Keep the recommended cell text, or use: "The issue author requested it. ### G06 [round-3 NEW finding, severity MINOR] Issue draft, applied as written, replaces the five existing Scope bullets
- Where in the round-3 design: 2. Proposed issue #4525 text (design-r3.txt:107, :109-118)
- Relates to earlier id(s): F19 F20 N42
- Kind: omission | found by lenses: fidelity, disposition-checker
- Claim: The block calls itself 'a draft replacement for the problem, terms, and scope paragraphs'. The live issue's Scope block has five bullets: use the existing expr interface; combine the address mapping with the concrete view and keep ownership separate; preserve existing layouts, conversions, initialization rules, optimized operations and cache behavior; type cache and kernel boundaries so callers cannot confuse packed V with native K; extend existing tests and verify they execute in required CI. The replacement paragraph at :118 carries only four sentences. Read literally, the edit removes the expr-precedent bullet from the reviewer's review 5270587330, the conversion and initialization-rules bullet that covers the reviewer's zero-initialization invariant, and the required-CI bullet that the design itself calls a repository rule (:468). Round-1 F19/F20 and round-2 N42 asked to ADD a problem paragraph, a terms list and one constraints bullet, not to replace the Scope list. The 'preserve' clause at :107 protects only the reviewer links and the PR-order list.
- The round-3 design text said: "This is a draft replacement for the problem, terms, and scope paragraphs. [...] Follow the reviewing maintainer's request for typed cache arguments. Construct the typed storage in the existing arena. Packed owners and views expose no public raw-pointer constructor, data member, or pointer conversion. Allocation must add no storage clearing."
- Evidence given in round-3: design-r3.txt:107, :109-118, :468. Live issue Scope bullets: gh issue view 4525 (2026-09-22) and evidence/github-review.json issue.body ('Scope:\n- Use the existing expr interface for logical tensor and row operations.\n- Combine the address mapping with the concrete view. Keep storage ownership separate.\n- Preserve existing layouts, conversions, initialization rules, optimized operations, and cache behavior.\n- Type cache and kernel boundaries so callers cannot confuse packed V with native K.\n- Extend relevant existing tests and verify they execute in required CI.'). Reviewer review 5270587330 (github-review.json comments[2].body): 'follow the precedent already present in tron: ... tensor and dtensor which provide a uniform interface based upon expr'. r2-findings.md N42 RECOMMENDATION ('a two-sentence problem paragraph ..., a Words-used-here list ..., one scope bullet with Ben's three constraints'); r1 F20 ('one scope bullet in issue #4525 restating the three constraints'). AGENTS.md@2880c3aa9b:46-48 (required-CI rule, via facts-r2.md). jhan's review request claude-review-add-tensor-type.md:9 ('Confirm issue #4525 captures the request(s)').
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): Change :107 to: 'This draft adds a problem paragraph and a terms list before the existing Scope block, and adds one Scope bullet. It replaces no existing Scope bullet and no PR-order item.' Move the four sentences at :118 into one added Scope bullet: '- Construct the typed storage inside the existing arena allocation, with no cast of the current SIMD array and no clearing added to allocation. Packed owners and views expose no public raw-pointer constructor, data member, or pointer conversion.' Keep the five existing Scope bullets verbatim.
- LEAD NOTE (final grade minor): Holds 3 of 3. The block calls itself a replacement for the scope paragraphs and names only the reviewer links and a "checklist" as things to keep, so a literal application drops the five Scope bullets, including the required-CI bullet and the initialization-rules bullet that covers Ben's zero-initialization invariant. Corrections: the repository rule is AGENTS.md:22-27, and four of the five bullets lose content, not three.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (source lens): Three corrections, none changes the verdict.

1. Fix the evidence citation for the repository rule: it is AGENTS.md@2880c3aa9b:22-27 ("Every committed test must run in CI. Before committing it, verify that a required CI suite collects and executes it"), not :46-48. Lines 46-48 hold the regression-test rule.

2. Reword the preserve-clause sentence. Replace "The 'preserve' clause at :107 protects only the reviewer links and the PR-order list" with: "The preserve clause at :107 names the reviewer links and 'the issue's remaining child-adaptation checklist'. The live issue has no checklist; its nearest match is the three-item PR-order list. The clause does not name the Scope bullets, and the term 'child-adaptation checklist' is defined nowhere in the design (it appears only at :107 and :120)."

3. Narrow the removal claim to cover both readings. Replace "Read literally, the edit removes ..." with: "Applied literally, the draft replaces the Scope block and so removes the expr-precedent bullet, the conversions-and-initialization-rules bullet, and the required-CI bullet. Under the alternative reading, where 'scope paragraphs' means the issue's first paragraph (which :111 rewrites), the five bullets survive but the four-sentence constraints paragraph at :118 has no placement instruction. In both readings the draft gives no instruction to keep the five bullets." Optionally add that the design's own section-9 rows describe the block as "scope text" [design-r3.txt:528, :547], which matches the replacement reading.

Also cite the live gh output as the primary source for the five Scope bullets and note that evidence/github-review.json issue.body is an older snapshot (pre-00:11:35Z wording) whose Scope bullets are identical.

Recommendation text stands as written.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (design-text lens): Keep the claim and the recommendation. Three refinements. (1) Widen the loss list: four of the five bullets lose content on a literal replacement, not three. Add the second bullet ("Combine the address mapping with the concrete view. Keep storage ownership separate."), which the draft does not restate; the design proposes exactly this in Q1 [design-r3.txt:96] but only inside the design. (2) Replace "protects only the reviewer links and the PR-order list" with: "names only the reviewer links and a 'child-adaptation checklist'; the live body (gh issue view 4525, 2026-09-22T00:11:35Z) has no checklist, so the nearest protected item is the 3-item 'PR order:' list, and the reader must guess". Recommend that :107 name the two lists literally: "Keep the Scope: list and the PR order: list. Add the bullet below as the sixth Scope item." (3) The constraints sentence "Construct the typed storage in the existing arena" [design-r3.txt:118] omits the mechanism F20 and N42 asked for, "no cast of the existing SIMD array" [r1-findings.md:496; r2-findings.md:636]. The finding's proposed bullet already carries that clause, so state this as a reason for the move, not as a separate finding. Citation fix: the finding cites github-review.json issue.body for the Scope bullets; that saved body predates the 00:11:35Z edit (it still has the old title suffix "before #4424" and the removed last sentence), but its five Scope bullets are identical to the live body, so the evidence stands with the live gh output as the primary cite.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (intent lens): Narrow the claim from "replaces" to "is ambiguous, and the literal reading replaces": the word "paragraphs" at design-r3.txt:107 could be read as excluding the bulleted Scope list, but the preamble names what to keep (reviewer links, child-adaptation checklist) and does not name the Scope list, so an applier cannot tell whether the five bullets survive. Three small fixes to the finding text: (1) The live issue has no "checklist"; it has a "PR order:" list of three numbered items [gh issue view 4525]. The correction to :107 and :120 should say "the PR order list", not "child-adaptation checklist". (2) The sentence at :118 "Follow the reviewing maintainer's request for typed cache arguments" carries no link [HTML blockquote, verified], while :107 says to preserve the reviewer links. The recommendation should state that the F21 change is only to replace the two words "Ben's" inside the existing "This follows ... [tensor-interface review] and [API sketch]" sentence and inside PR-order item 3, keeping the three links unchanged, and that the four-sentence paragraph becomes one added Scope bullet. (3) Add a disposition consequence: the section-9 rows for N42 [design-r3.txt:528] and F19 F20 F21 [design-r3.txt:547] should be graded "partly", not "resolved", because the recommendation asked for additions and the design text specifies a replacement. Keep severity minor; the recommendation's proposed wording for :107 ("This draft adds a problem paragraph and a terms list before the existing Scope block, and adds one Scope bullet. It replaces no existing Scope bullet and no PR-order item.") is correct and sufficient.

### G07 [round-3 NEW finding, severity MINOR] Issue has no 'child-adaptation checklist'; it has a PR order list
- Where in the round-3 design: 2. Proposed issue #4525 text (design-r3.txt:107, :120)
- Relates to earlier id(s): M06
- Kind: factual error | found by lenses: fidelity
- Claim: The block twice refers to 'the issue's remaining child-adaptation checklist' and makes the closing rule depend on it. The live issue body has no checklist (no task-list items). It has a 'Scope:' bullet list and a numbered 'PR order:' list whose items 2 and 3 describe the child adaptation. An assignee applying the text, or closing the issue by the rule at :120, would look for a structure that does not exist. The round-2 M06 verifier already warned that the three ordered items are the PR order list, not a scope or checklist.
- The round-3 design text said: "Preserve existing reviewer links and the issue's remaining child-adaptation checklist. [...] The assignee closes the issue after its child-adaptation checklist is complete, or explicitly splits that work into another issue."
- Evidence given in round-3: design-r3.txt:107 and :120. Live issue body (gh issue view 4525 -R positron-ai/tron, 2026-09-22): 'Scope:' with five '- ' bullets and 'PR order:' with items '1. Add the abstraction for existing V and native K on main. 2. Rebase #4424 onto that parent and add its packed-K representation. 3. Keep attention algorithm changes and shared-save coordination in #4424. ...'; no '- [ ]' or '- [x]' item. Saved body in evidence/github-review.json issue.body (retrieved 2026-09-21 23:35:57 UTC) has the same two lists. r2-findings.md M06 intent-lens correction: 'the Scope bullets cover the parent only; the three items are the PR order list.'
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): At :107 replace 'the issue's remaining child-adaptation checklist' with 'the issue's PR order list; its items 2 and 3 describe the child adaptation'. At :120 replace 'after its child-adaptation checklist is complete' with 'after PR order items 2 and 3 are complete (the child is rebased onto the merged parent and its packed-K representation uses the new types)'.
- LEAD NOTE (final grade minor): Holds 3 of 3 (one verifier at note). The live issue has a Scope list and a PR order list and no checklist. Both sentences are the design's instructions to the assignee, outside the paste block, so they do not enter the issue. Correction to the recommendation: PR order item 3 is a placement rule with no completion state, so the closing rule should name item 2 only.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (source lens): Keep the claim and the :107 replacement. Narrow the :120 replacement. PR order item 3 ("Keep attention algorithm changes and shared-save coordination in #4424") is a placement rule with no completion state, so "after PR order items 2 and 3 are complete" cannot be checked. Suggested :120 text: "The assignee closes the issue after PR order item 2 is done (#4424 is rebased onto the merged parent and its packed-K representation uses the new types), or explicitly splits that work into another issue. Item 3 is a placement rule for #4424, not a task to complete." Also state in the finding that the two sentences are the design's instructions to the assignee, not part of the blockquoted text to paste into the issue [design-new-tensor-type.html, section "issue-proposal"], and that the wording is new in round 3 [design-wdiff.txt:333-346].
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (design-text lens): Two narrowings. (1) The evidence sentence "The round-2 M06 verifier already warned that the three ordered items are the PR order list, not a scope or checklist" overstates the correction. The intent-lens text says only: "the Scope bullets cover the parent only; the three items are the 'PR order' list" [r2-findings.md:583]. It does not mention "checklist". Reword to: "already noted that the three ordered items are the PR order list, not the Scope list." (2) The recommendation for :120 says "after PR order items 2 and 3 are complete". Item 3 ("Keep attention algorithm changes and shared-save coordination in #4424") is a placement rule, not a task with its own completion point. Suggested wording for :120: "The assignee closes the issue after PR order item 2 is done, that is, after #4424 is rebased onto the merged parent with its packed-K representation on the new types and #4424 merges with the item-3 work, or explicitly splits that work into another issue." The :107 recommendation stands; also consider dropping "remaining", since item 1 of the PR order list is not done when the text is applied: "Preserve existing reviewer links and the PR order list; its items 2 and 3 describe the child adaptation."
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (intent lens): Narrow the claim to: "The design's instructions to the assignee at design-r3.txt:107 and :120 call the issue's 'PR order:' list a 'child-adaptation checklist'. The live issue has no checklist and no task-list items [gh issue view 4525, 2026-09-22]. Replace "The round-2 M06 verifier already warned that the three ordered items are the PR order list, not a scope or checklist" with "The round-2 M06 intent-lens correction named the three items 'the PR order list' and separated them from the Scope bullets; the word 'checklist' first appears in this revision [design-wdiff.txt:335, :343-344]." Drop "would look for a structure that does not exist" and say instead "must infer that the PR order list is meant, and whether item 1 (the parent) is kept". Keep the recommendation's two wording changes at :107 and :120, and add: keep both sentences outside the blockquote, as they are now. Kind: mislabel (ambiguity), not a factual error that misleads implementation. Severity: note.

### G08 [round-3 NEW finding, severity MINOR] Forward declarations of bf16, view and const_view should be spelled with the struct key
- Where in the round-3 design: 5. Interface design, declarations header paragraph (design-r3.txt:234; block at :232)
- Relates to earlier id(s): F43 N14 N20 N22
- Kind: omission | found by lenses: cpp-interface, handoff, simplicity, regression-diff, disposition-checker
- Claim: The new sentence tells the implementer to forward-declare bf16, view and const_view as 'classes'. All three are defined with the struct key. A forward declaration with the class key compiles, but clang 19 (the pinned compiler) emits -Wmismatched-tags under -Wall in every translation unit that sees both the forward declaration and the definition. The header is included by amx_attn_iface.hpp and so by most of tron. No -Werror is set, so this is warning noise, not a build break. The existing forward declarations in h/tron/kernels/expr.hpp:17-22 use struct and are the pattern to copy. bf16 is already complete wherever amx_attn_iface.hpp is included, because that header includes the intrinsics-free common/numerics/bf16.hpp, so a bf16 forward declaration adds nothing. The writable native_k_view alias in the same block has no parent caller.
- The round-3 design text said: "The new declarations header also forward-declares the actual bf16, view, and const_view classes in their existing namespaces. Declare all five view parameters without defaults."
- Evidence given in round-3: h/common/numerics/bf16.hpp@2880c3aa9b:10-12 (namespace tron { ... struct bf16 {), :3-8 (includes only <cmath>, <cstdlib>, <type_traits>, common/attributes.hpp, common/numerics/std.hpp). h/tron/tensor/views.hpp@2880c3aa9b:22-27 (template <typename T, bool aligned, bool dma, typename Dim, typename Stride = row_major<Dim>> struct view {), :31-36 (struct const_view;). h/tron/tensor/view.hpp@2880c3aa9b:25, :116, :263, :276 (struct specializations). h/tron/kernels/expr.hpp@2880c3aa9b:17-22 (forward declarations with struct). h/tron/kernels/amx_attn_iface.hpp@2880c3aa9b:112 (#include "common/numerics/bf16.hpp"), :2-5 (no-intrinsics rule). CMakeLists.txt@2880c3aa9b:608-611 (-Wall;-Wpedantic;-Wextra); git grep -i werror at 2880c3aa9b: only GNUmakefile:704 (clang-format --dry-run -Werror); flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:589-594 and nix/cmake-tron-test-build.nix@2880c3aa9b:3,13 (clang 19). Clang's -Wall includes -Wmost, which includes -Wmismatched-tags (compiler documentation). facts-r3.md:68 records the same. The writable alias native_k_view (design-r3.txt:232) is used nowhere else in the design; the kernel takes const_native_k_view (:277) and kv_block::k_view spells its own type (kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2208-2211). design-r3.txt:234 is a NEW round-3 segment (design-wdiff.txt: REPLACE @old 2305 / new 3179).
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): Replace the first sentence of design-r3.txt:234 with: 'The declarations header includes common/numerics/bf16.hpp (intrinsics-free; amx_attn_iface.hpp already includes it) and forward-declares the primary templates with the struct key their definitions use, exactly as h/tron/kernels/expr.hpp:17-22 does: template <typename T, bool aligned, bool dma, typename Dim, typename Stride> struct view; and the same for const_view, in namespace tron, with no default arguments.' In the declarations block (:232) drop the writable native_k_view alias, or add 'declared for symmetry; no parent caller uses it'.
- LEAD NOTE (final grade minor): Holds 3 of 3 as an omission, not a factual error: the design says "classes" and gives no declaration text, while its own block uses the class key for the new types. All three types are structs; clang 19 under -Wall warns (-Wmismatched-tags) in every translation unit that sees both, and no -Werror is set, so this is warning noise, not a build break. Round-2 N14's corrected recommendation already spelled struct view; and struct const_view; as expr.hpp:17-22 does. Drop the sub-claim that the bf16 declaration is redundant: under the design's own include rule the header must forward-declare bf16.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (source lens): Narrow the finding as follows. Title: "Declarations header does not state the class-key for bf16, view and const_view; their definitions use struct". Kind: ambiguity/omission (the design names them "classes" but prescribes no key; its own block at design-r3.txt:232 uses `class` for the new types, which an implementer may copy). Drop the sub-claim "the bf16 declaration is redundant": under the design's rule "Include only the standard headers needed for sizes and integer sequences" [design-r3.txt:234] the header must forward-declare bf16 to name it in the aliases at :232. Replace the recommendation's first sentence with: "Forward-declare `struct bf16;` in namespace tron [h/common/numerics/bf16.hpp@2880c3aa9b:10-12] and `template <typename T, bool aligned, bool dma, typename Dim, typename Stride> struct view;` and the same for `const_view`, with the struct key their definitions use [h/tron/tensor/views.hpp@2880c3aa9b:22-36] and no default arguments, exactly as h/tron/kernels/expr.hpp@2880c3aa9b:17-22 does. A `class` key compiles but triggers clang's -Wmismatched-tags (in -Wmost, in -Wall; CMakeLists.txt@2880c3aa9b:608-611) in every translation unit that includes amx_attn_iface.hpp. Alternatively include common/numerics/bf16.hpp, which is intrinsics-free (its includes are <cmath>, <cstdlib>, <type_traits>, common/attributes.hpp, common/numerics/std.hpp) and which amx_attn_iface.hpp already includes at :112; that replaces the bf16 forward declaration but relaxes the standard-headers-only rule at :234." Suggested optional wording only: "native_k_view is declared for symmetry with const_native_k_view; no parent caller uses it."
