### G30 [round-3 NEW finding, severity MINOR] Typed-boundary check row dropped the explicit kernel-call rejections that prove the kernel boundary
- Where in the round-3 design: 8. Behavior checks, Typed function boundaries row (design-r3.txt:438); 9. row M03 (:530)
- Relates to earlier id(s): M03
- Kind: omission | found by lenses: disposition-checker
- Claim: The fix replaced the round-2 sentence 'Reject raw pointers and packed V at native-K kernel calls; reject native K at packed-V calls' with 'Reject implicit raw-pointer arguments and wrong packed view types'. In the parent the two kernel cache-plane parameters are const_native_k_view<PAGE_TOKENS_64, HEAD_SIZE_128> and v_vnni_view<const bf16, PAGE_TOKENS_64, HEAD_SIZE_128>. A native K view passed to weights_times_v_128x4 is not a 'packed view type' at all, and a packed V view passed to qk_rowmajor_128x4 is not a 'wrong packed view' either, so the new wording no longer asks for the two assertions that prove the kernel boundary itself, which is the reviewer's request #2.
- The round-3 design text said: "Use dependent requires concepts and STATIC_REQUIRE / STATIC_REQUIRE_FALSE in the existing suite. Reject implicit raw-pointer arguments and wrong packed view types. Check read-only writes, const-row mutation, reverse const conversion, and rvalue-owner views. Accept writable-to-read-only conversion. Keep explicit native-view pointer construction legal."
- Evidence given in round-3: evidence/claude-review-r2/design-revised-text.txt:397 and design-wdiff.txt:1576-1580 (OLD sentence). design-r3.txt:277 (typed signatures), :68 (reviewer request #2), :438, :530. h/tron/kernels/amx_attn_iface.hpp@2880c3aa9b:219-222 and :230-233 (today both plane parameters are const bf16*). Declarations visible in t_llama_unit through model.hpp:53 -> self_attention.hpp:21 -> amx_attn_iface.hpp at 2880c3aa9b.
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): At :438 add: 'Assert with STATIC_REQUIRE_FALSE that qk_rowmajor_128x4 rejects a v_vnni_view and a bf16*, and that weights_times_v_128x4 rejects a const_native_k_view and a bf16*; the declarations are already visible in t_llama_unit through model.hpp -> self_attention.hpp -> amx_attn_iface.hpp (2880c3aa9b: model.hpp:53, self_attention.hpp:21).'
- LEAD NOTE (final grade minor): Holds 3 of 3 as lost specificity in the M03 fix, not a factual error: "wrong packed view types" can cover a v_vnni_view passed to qk_rowmajor_128x4, but a const_native_k_view passed to weights_times_v_128x4 is not a packed view at all, and the row names no function. "Kernel arguments" is the design's own restatement of Ben's request, not his words.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (source lens): Four narrowings. (1) Kind: "omission (regression of the round-2 row text)", not "factual error"; nothing in the new sentence is false, it is under-specified. (2) Narrow the claim: "wrong packed view types" can be read to cover a v_vnni_view passed to qk_rowmajor_128x4 (a packed view of the wrong type), so drop "is not a 'wrong packed view' either"; keep that it does not cover a const_native_k_view passed to weights_times_v_128x4 (native K is not a packed view [design-r3.txt:23]), and that neither reading names the two kernel functions as the check site, which the round-2 row did [design-revised-text.txt:397]. (3) Replace "which is the reviewer's request #2" with "which the design itself lists as part of request #2 [design-r3.txt:68, :73]"; Ben's review 5270587330 says only that bf16_t* values must not float around without a layout indication and does not mention kernels [evidence/github-review.json, comments[2]]. Disposition note: M03 is otherwise addressed (mechanism, case, get_v concept [design-r3.txt:438, :530]); G30 is a new minor omission introduced while applying N22, not a "regressed" M03. (4) Refine the recommendation to match the row's own mechanism ("dependent requires concepts") and the existing constants at t/t_llama_unit.cpp@2880c3aa9b:84-123: add to :438 "Write two dependent requires-expression constants templated on the plane argument type, next to the existing constants, and assert in the case 'attention operations resolve to typed KV slots' (:316): STATIC_REQUIRE_FALSE for qk_rowmajor_128x4 with bf16* and with v_vnni_view<const bf16, 64, 128>; STATIC_REQUIRE_FALSE for weights_times_v_128x4 with bf16* and with const_native_k_view<64, 128>; STATIC_REQUIRE for each function with its own typed view. The declarations are unconditional in amx_attn_iface.hpp (namespace tron::amx_attn_h128g4) and already reach t_llama_unit through t_llama_unit.cpp:66 -> model.hpp:53 -> self_attention.hpp:21; the full V definitions arrive through kv_cache.hpp (t_llama_unit.cpp:65)." Also mention the same two sites in the section-9 M03 row (:530) so the table matches the body.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (design-text lens): Change the kind from "factual error" to "omission (round-2 specificity lost)". Narrow the claim: under a broad reading, "Reject implicit raw-pointer arguments and wrong packed view types" [design-r3.txt:438] can cover three of the four kernel assertions (a bf16* at either kernel, a v_vnni_view at qk_rowmajor_128x4), but the row names no function, and the fourth assertion (a const_native_k_view at weights_times_v_128x4) is outside the sentence because a native K view is not a packed view type. Keep the recommendation, with this citation form: "At design-r3.txt:438 add: 'Assert with STATIC_REQUIRE_FALSE that qk_rowmajor_128x4 rejects a v_vnni_view<const bf16, PAGE_TOKENS_64, HEAD_SIZE_128> and an implicit bf16*, and that weights_times_v_128x4 rejects a const_native_k_view<PAGE_TOKENS_64, HEAD_SIZE_128> and a bf16*. The declarations are already visible in t_llama_unit (t/t_llama_unit.cpp@2880c3aa9b:66 includes tron/models/model.hpp; model.hpp:53 includes self_attention.hpp; self_attention.hpp:21 includes amx_attn_iface.hpp).'" Record M03 as resolved and G30 as a new minor residual, not as an M03 regression.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (intent lens): Three narrowings. (1) Kind: this is not a "factual error". The new sentence is true but less specific than the round-2 sentence. Classify it as lost specificity in the M03 fix (M03 = partly). The M03 corrected recommendation asked for the mechanism and the case, and its intent-lens correction presupposed the kernel-call rejections would stay ("kernel-call rejections need the amx_attn_iface.hpp declarations included in t_llama_unit", r2-findings.md M03). The design supplied the mechanism (dependent requires concepts plus STATIC_REQUIRE / STATIC_REQUIRE_FALSE, design-r3.txt:438) and dropped the two explicit kernel checks in the same edit. (2) Attribution: Ben's review 5270587330 says pointers "provide no hints as to the layout" and "we really don't want bf16_t* values floating around" [evidence/github-review.json]. "Kernel arguments" is the design's own restatement of request #2 [design-r3.txt:68]. Reword "which is the reviewer's request #2" to "which the design's restatement of request #2 at :68 promises". (3) Recommendation additions: the kernel names live in namespace tron::amx_attn_h128g4 [amx_attn_iface.hpp@2880c3aa9b:114-240] and are unguarded, so the requires-expression must qualify them. The design already keeps the typed declarations available at both chunk widths [design-r3.txt:281], so the check compiles in both builds. const_view's pointer constructor is already explicit [h/tron/tensor/view.hpp@2880c3aa9b:296, :373], so "qk_rowmajor_128x4 rejects a bf16*" needs no view.hpp change and agrees with "Keep explicit native-view pointer construction legal" [design-r3.txt:438] and "The alias still permits an explicit ordinary pointer wrap" [:267]. The v_vnni_view side depends on the parent making its constructors restricted, which the design already requires [design-r3.txt:75 "restricted construction of views"]. Suggested row text: "Assert with STATIC_REQUIRE_FALSE that qk_rowmajor_128x4 rejects a v_vnni_view<const bf16, 64, 128> and a const bf16*, and that weights_times_v_128x4 rejects a const_native_k_view<64, 128> and a const bf16*. Write each as a dependent requires-expression constant next to the existing constants in t_llama_unit.cpp; the declarations are visible there through model.hpp:53 -> self_attention.hpp:21 -> amx_attn_iface.hpp (2880c3aa9b)."

### G31 [round-3 NEW finding, severity MINOR] Section 9 row 538 hides the missing objdump inspection item for F38 (the V row unpack)
- Where in the round-3 design: 9. Disposition, round-1 rows (design-r3.txt:538, :542); body 8. Instruction comparison (:486)
- Relates to earlier id(s): F38 F35 M05
- Kind: omission | found by lenses: section9-audit
- Claim: The round-1 residuals F38 and F35 asked section 8's instruction comparison to inspect two more code regions: the V row unpack (page::get_v -> load_row, called from full.hpp's v_head_fn) and the exact bf16 row reads (bf16_chunk). The body's inspection list at :486 names only even/odd V append, packed token copy, weighted sums, and allocation/restore instantiations. The remaining cell of row 538 says 'All changes are still implementation work' and row 542 says 'Packed-body compilation and exact signature checks remain to run'. Neither cell names the two missing inspection items, so the table reads as if F38 and F35 were fully carried.
- The round-3 design text said: "Locate the enclosing Catch test functions through source lines for even/odd V append, packed token copy, and weighted sums. Locate surviving allocation/restore instantiations. Forced-inline helpers may have no separate symbol."
- Evidence given in round-3: design-r3.txt:486; grep of design-r3.txt lines 480-495 for 'unpack', 'load_row', 'bf16_chunk' returns no line; in the HTML the performance section spans lines 411-420 and the nine occurrences of unpack/load_row/bf16_chunk sit at HTML lines 195-374, outside it. r2-findings.md F38 STILL NEEDED ('add the V row unpack to the inspected call sites ... page::get_v -> load_row, one _mm512_cvtepi32_epi16 per 16 dimensions today'); r2-findings.md F35 STILL NEEDED ('add exact bf16 row reads (bf16_chunk) to the inspected regions'). design-r3.txt:538, :542.
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): At design-r3.txt:486 write: 'Locate the enclosing Catch test functions through source lines for even/odd V append, packed token copy, the V row unpack (page::get_v -> load_row, called from full.hpp's v_head_fn), exact bf16 row reads (bf16_chunk), and weighted sums.' Until that edit lands, change the row-538 remaining cell to 'F38: the V row unpack is not yet in the section 8 inspection list' and the row-542 cell to 'F35: bf16_chunk reads are not yet in the section 8 inspection list'.
- LEAD NOTE (final grade minor): Holds 3 of 3 for F38 and is refuted for F35 by two verifiers: the decltype check at :440 detects a missing bf16_chunk override at compile time, so adding bf16_chunk to the objdump list is not needed and row 542 describes that detector correctly. Correction to the recommendation: name the get_v call sites inside t_llama_unit (for example :819, :1575, :2038), not full.hpp's v_head_fn, which is in a different binary.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (source lens): Three corrections. (a) The recommended wording "the V row unpack (page::get_v -> load_row, called from full.hpp's v_head_fn)" repeats text the round-2 source re-grader already corrected (batches/B09.md; also on the round-2 votes page): the comparison runs objdump on the two gen/t_llama_unit binaries (design-r3.txt:486), and t/t_llama_unit.cpp@2880c3aa9b includes neither full.hpp nor v_head_fn (git grep finds no full.hpp, make_page_info or v_head_fn in that file), so the full.hpp caller is in a different binary. Reword to: "the V row unpack (page::get_v -> load_row, one _mm512_cvtepi32_epi16 per 16 dimensions today, kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2454 and :2462) at its t_llama_unit call sites, for example t/t_llama_unit.cpp@2880c3aa9b:819, :1575, :2038 (also :107 inside a requires-expression, :2539, :2761, :2824)". (b) Narrow the F35 half to a note. The design added a compile-time detector for the missing override: design-r3.txt:440 asks to "Check decltype(&Row::bf16_chunk) against bf16s (Row::*)(size_t) const noexcept to distinguish the derived override from an inherited fallback" and says "Bit patterns alone cannot detect the bit-exact fallback." Taking the address of an inherited member yields a pointer-to-member of the base class, so that check fails whenever Row lacks its own bf16_chunk, and a wrong signature already fails to compile at the base's member-pointer comparison (h/tron/kernels/expr.hpp@2880c3aa9b:111-113). So the objdump inspection of bf16_chunk is no longer the only detector, and the literal item is not actionable as written: the test row at :440 builds a host tensor, and host tensors read rows through chunk() only (round-2 source re-grader, tensor.hpp@f46e48bab498:81-85, 125-129), so a t_llama_unit binary would hold no bf16_chunk region to inspect unless the test calls row.bf16_chunk(i) directly or assigns the row to a bf16 dmatensor. Row 542 is therefore not hiding a live gap; at most it could say "F35: the decltype check at section 8 replaces the objdump inspection of bf16_chunk reads". (c) Evidence slip: the HTML holds 10 occurrences of unpack/load_row/bf16_chunk on 9 lines (195, 204, 211, 221 twice, 273, 299, 315, 345, 374), not nine occurrences; and the "Inspect vector extraction/merge and vdpbf16ps" sentence is design-r3.txt:488, not :486. The F38 half stands as a minor omission at design-r3.txt:486 with row 538 not naming it.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (design-text lens): Narrow G31 to F38 and drop the F35 half. Suggested title: "Section 8 objdump region list omits the V row unpack carried as F38's round-2 residual". Suggested claim: "The round-2 page carried F38 as Partly with one residual: add the V row unpack to the objdump region list. The round-3 list at design-r3.txt:486 still names only even/odd V append, packed token copy, weighted sums, and allocation/restore. Row 538's remaining cell does not name the omission. F35's parallel item is superseded: design-r3.txt:440 detects a missing bf16_chunk override with a compile-time decltype check, and row 542 names that check."

Fix the recommendation's mechanism per the round-2 source re-grader correction [r2-findings.md:819]: the objdump target is gen/t_llama_unit, whose translation unit does not include full.hpp, so name the test call sites, not v_head_fn. Suggested text for design-r3.txt:486: "Locate the enclosing Catch test functions through source lines for even/odd V append, packed token copy, weighted sums, and the V row unpack (today's bf16 page::get_v loops, one _mm512_cvtepi32_epi16 per 16 dimensions [h/tron/models/kv_cache.hpp@f46e48bab498:2454, :2462]; the parent's load_row) at its t_llama_unit bf16-destination call sites [t/t_llama_unit.cpp@2880c3aa9b:107, :819, :1575]. The production unpack caller, full.hpp's v_head_fn [h/tron/scheduler/full.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2652-2657], is in a different binary and is not compared here." Keep the row-538 cell edit as optional: "F38: the V row unpack is not yet in the section 8 region list." Do not change row 542. Optional note-level addition, not required: add "exact bf16 row reads (bf16_chunk)" to :486 for instruction-level evidence that the override compiles to a direct extraction; detection of a missing override is already covered by the decltype check at :440.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (intent lens): Narrow the finding to F38 only and retitle it: "Section 9 row 538 hides the missing objdump inspection item for F38 (V row unpack)". Drop every F35 statement: the decltype(&Row::bf16_chunk) check at design-r3.txt:440 is a compile-time detector of a missing override (verified by compile test), so adding bf16_chunk to the objdump list is not needed, and row 542's "exact signature checks remain to run" [design-r3.txt:542] describes that detector correctly.

Replace the recommendation with: At design-r3.txt:486 write "Locate the enclosing Catch test functions through source lines for even/odd V append, packed token copy, the V row unpack (page::get_v -> load_row at its t_llama_unit call sites, for example t/t_llama_unit.cpp:819, :1575, :2038 at 2880c3aa9b; today one _mm512_cvtepi32_epi16 per 16 dimensions, kv_cache.hpp@f46e48bab498:2454 and :2462), and weighted sums." Do not name full.hpp's v_head_fn as an inspection site: objdump runs on gen/t_llama_unit, and t/t_llama_unit.cpp@2880c3aa9b does not include full.hpp. Until that edit lands, change the row-538 remaining cell to "F38: the V row unpack is not yet in the section 8 inspection list" [design-r3.txt:538]. Leave row 542 unchanged. Keep relates_to as "F38 M05" and severity minor.

### G34 [round-3 NEW finding, severity MINOR] S18 labels for the two generated save calls are swapped
- Where in the round-3 design: 10. Sources and saved files, S18 row (design-r3.txt:582; design-r3-links.tsv lines 223-224)
- Relates to earlier id(s): F29 N33
- Kind: factual error | found by lenses: child-needs, disposition-checker
- Claim: The S18 link named 'Main generated save call' points at ingest/src/TronCpp.hs lines 2337-2340, which is the helper-side save_k_helper emission inside runHelperStatement (the function whose output run_main_help concatenates). The link named 'Helper generated save call' points at lines 2405-2408, which is the run() save_k emission inside submitAttentionOperation/saveKv, whose comment says n_workers is in scope in run(). The two labels describe each other's target. The body sentence at :424 has the roles right; S18 did not exist in round 2, so the fix for N33 introduced the mislabel.
- The round-3 design text said: "[Both worker API paths] [Main generated save call] [Helper generated save call] [Generated-call expectations] [Direct worker test calls]"
- Evidence given in round-3: design-r3-links.tsv line 223 ([Main generated save call] -> ingest/src/TronCpp.hs#L2337-L2340), line 224 ([Helper generated save call] -> #L2405-L2408), both at 30c4ac82cb. ingest/src/TronCpp.hs@30c4ac82cb:2292 (runHelperStatement), :2324-2340 (comment 'The helpers take part in the K store'; attentionRuntimeCall "save_k_helper" attention [..., ExprLit "worker_ix", ExprLit "n_workers"] at 2338-2340), :1281 (run_main_help concatenates it), :2370 (submitAttentionOperation), :2400-2408 (comment 'n_workers (main + main helpers) is in scope in run()'; attentionRuntimeCall "save_k" stmt [..., ExprLit "n_workers"] at 2405-2408). design-r3.txt:424 ('Generated run() passes 0, n_workers. Generated run_main_help passes worker_ix, n_workers.'). evidence/claude-review-r2/design-revised-text.txt: no 'S18'.
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): Swap the two hrefs so that [Main generated save call] points at ingest/src/TronCpp.hs#L2402-L2408 (save_k in submitAttentionOperation) and [Helper generated save call] points at #L2333-L2340 (save_k_helper in runHelperStatement).
- LEAD NOTE (final grade minor): Holds 3 of 3 with no narrowing. The minimal fix swaps the two hrefs (or the two label texts). N33 and F29 are partly because of this slip.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (source lens): Keep the finding as written. Two small additions. (1) Complete the call chain for the main side: submitAttentionOperation is reached from runStatement's AttentionStmt case [ingest/src/TronCpp.hs@30c4ac82cb:2277, declared 2230-2236], and run() concatenates runStatement at 1178 [same file:1169-1178]. (2) The recommendation's wider ranges are correct: 2402-2408 covers the run()-side comment plus the save_k call [same file:2402-2408], and 2333-2340 covers the AttentionStmt case head plus the save_k_helper call [same file:2333-2340]. Swapping the two existing hrefs unchanged (2337-2340 and 2405-2408) is an equally correct fix. For the section-9 disposition of N33, the lead may record "resolved in the body, one label slip in the S18 source row", which fits the "partly" definition (a small factual slip remains) if the lead applies it strictly.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (design-text lens): The claim, evidence, and citations are right and need no narrowing. One small correction to the recommendation: the minimal fix is to swap the two link TEXTS and keep the existing hrefs (#L2337-L2340 stays the helper call, #L2405-L2408 stays the main call). Swapping the hrefs with the widened ranges the finding proposes (#L2402-L2408 to include the "n_workers ... in scope in run()" comment at 2402-2404, and #L2333-L2340 to include the AttentionStmt case arm at 2333-2336) is also correct and slightly more informative, but not required. Either way the labels must read: "[Main generated save call]" -> the `"save_k"` emission in submitAttentionOperation [ingest/src/TronCpp.hs@30c4ac82cb:2405-2408]; "[Helper generated save call]" -> the `"save_k_helper"` emission in runHelperStatement [ingest/src/TronCpp.hs@30c4ac82cb:2337-2340].
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (intent lens): No narrowing of the claim is needed. Two small precisions for the recommendation. (1) The simplest fix is to swap the two hrefs and keep the existing ranges: [Main generated save call] -> #L2405-L2408 (the save_k call) and [Helper generated save call] -> #L2337-L2340 (the save_k_helper call). (2) The wider ranges the finding proposes are also accurate: #L2402-L2408 adds the three comment lines that name run() [TronCpp.hs@30c4ac82cb:2402-2404], and #L2333-L2340 adds the AttentionStmt case head and the Nothing/Just arms [TronCpp.hs@30c4ac82cb:2333-2336]. Either form is correct; the design should pick one and keep the label-to-site pairing consistent with design-r3.txt:424.

### G49 [round-3 NEW finding, severity MINOR] AMX-on row asks for 'evidence that the kernel ran' without naming a method
- Where in the round-3 design: 8. Build configurations table, row '16 lanes, AMX code on' (design-r3.txt:453; rule at :445)
- Kind: omission | found by lenses: process-ci
- Claim: The row requires 'evidence that the kernel ran' for real numerical results but names no artifact that counts. The design's own rule at :445 says hardware-unavailable early returns do not count as execution. The AMX tests print a WARN line and return when the runtime probe says AMX is unavailable, and the probe also returns false when TRON_AMX_DISABLE=1 is set. A concrete, source-backed method exists and should be named; otherwise the implementer may present a green t_amx_numerics run from a host without AMX as kernel execution.
- The round-3 design text said: "Compile real kernels and unconditional fake definitions. Execute applicable fake dispatch cases. Real numerical results require compatible AMX hardware and evidence that the kernel ran."
- Evidence given in round-3: t/t_amx_numerics.cpp@2880c3aa9b:146, :190 (WARN("AMX unavailable on this host - QK precision check skipped") and the PV counterpart before returning); t/t_amx_numerics.cpp@30c4ac82cb:199-202 (same pattern for the VNNI QK check). h/tron/kernels/amx_attn_iface.hpp@2880c3aa9b:167-170 and src/tron/kernels/amx_attn.cpp@2880c3aa9b:61 (available() true when CPU, OS and per-process permission allow AMX and TRON_AMX_DISABLE is not 1). design-r3.txt:445, :453.
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): After 'evidence that the kernel ran' add: 'Evidence is the Slice log of t_amx_numerics and t_amx_dispatch_dtype from delphi-3bda showing the AMX precision sections passed with no AMX unavailable on this host WARN line, plus a control run of the same binaries with TRON_AMX_DISABLE=1 whose log shows the WARN lines. Record both log paths.'
- LEAD NOTE (final grade minor): The finder graded this a note; all three verifiers grade it minor and the lead follows them. A "Catch2 passed" line proves nothing here: both AMX precision cases return before any assertion when available() is false, after a WARN line, and the listener logs passed whenever no assertion failed (testlog.cpp:21-25). Correction: drop t_amx_dispatch_dtype from the kernel-ran evidence; it links CPU fakes and a fake available() that returns true. Name the t_amx_numerics Slice log with no "AMX unavailable" WARN line plus a TRON_AMX_DISABLE=1 control run.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (source lens): Narrow the recommendation to t_amx_numerics only. t_amx_dispatch_dtype defines its own fake `bool available() noexcept { return true; }` and fake kernels, and its header says "no AMX instruction is linked or executed; available() is the fake too" [t/t_amx_dispatch_dtype.cpp@2880c3aa9b:4-8, 166-170]. It therefore never prints the "AMX unavailable" WARN, and TRON_AMX_DISABLE=1 has no effect on it. The design row already covers it correctly as "Execute applicable fake dispatch cases" [design-r3.txt:453]. Fix citations: the TRON_AMX_DISABLE read is in detect_and_request [src/tron/kernels/amx_attn.cpp@2880c3aa9b:58-62], not in available() itself [:76-86]; the child's WARN sites are :146-148 (QK), :199-201 (VNNI QK) and :247-249 (PV) at 30c4ac82cb. Reworded recommendation: after "evidence that the kernel ran" add: "Evidence is the Slice run log (logs-<host>/run-NNNN/t_amx_numerics.log) from delphi-3bda in which the cases 'AMX QK does not digress from the AVX dotter' and 'AMX PV stays inside the AVX weight-rounding envelope' show Catch2 passed and no 'AMX unavailable on this host' WARN line, plus a control run of the same binary with TRON_AMX_DISABLE=1 whose log shows those WARN lines. 'Catch2 passed' alone is not enough: the tests WARN and return when available() is false, and that still passes. t_amx_dispatch_dtype uses fake kernels and is not hardware evidence." The parent row can reuse the runtime TRON_AMX_DISABLE=1 control the design already prescribes for the child [design-r3.txt:443]. Also add the WARN-line rule as a sentence under :469 so the CI-evidence paragraph and the :445 rule agree.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (design-text lens): Narrow the recommendation to t_amx_numerics and drop t_amx_dispatch_dtype. Suggested design text after "evidence that the kernel ran" (design-r3.txt:453): "Evidence is the t_amx_numerics output from delphi-3bda in which the two AMX precision cases (QK vs the AVX dotter; PV weight-rounding envelope) end with Catch2 passed and contain no 'AMX unavailable on this host' WARN line [t/t_amx_numerics.cpp@2880c3aa9b:145-147, 189-191], plus a control run of the same binary with TRON_AMX_DISABLE=1 whose output shows both WARN lines [src/tron/kernels/amx_attn.cpp@2880c3aa9b:61-62]. A Catch2 passed line alone is not enough: the listener logs passed whenever no assertion failed, which is also true after the early return [t/testlog.cpp@2880c3aa9b:21-25]. Record both output paths. t_amx_dispatch_dtype is not kernel-ran evidence: it links CPU fakes for every entry point and its fake available() returns true regardless of hardware or TRON_AMX_DISABLE [t/t_amx_dispatch_dtype.cpp@2880c3aa9b:5-8, 168-170]." For the child, add the third case (VNNI QK identity, t/t_amx_numerics.cpp@30c4ac82cb:199-201) to the same rule. Also fix the S13 label at design-r3.txt:577: "[AMX availability check]" is marked "Child:" but the same early return exists on main at 2880c3aa9b:145-147 and 189-191, so the sentence should read "Main and child: the AMX numerical test returns early ...". Whether the Slice log captures the Catch2 reporter's WARN output (as opposed to the test binary's own stdout) was not verified here; the design should say "test output or Slice log" until that is checked.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (intent lens): Narrow the recommendation in three ways. (1) Drop t_amx_dispatch_dtype from the kernel-execution evidence. That test defines available() as a fake returning true and defines all three kernels as CPU fakes; its header says no AMX instruction is linked or executed [t/t_amx_dispatch_dtype.cpp@2880c3aa9b:4-8, 168-170, 174-185]. It has no "AMX unavailable" WARN line. Its passed sections prove fake dispatch, which the row already covers under "Execute applicable fake dispatch cases" [design-r3.txt:453]. (2) Name the artifact precisely: the Slice run log logs-<host>/run-NNNN/t_amx_numerics.log, which holds the test process's stdout and stderr [bin/slice@2880c3aa9b:2770, 3148, 3174, 3182-3187], must contain "Catch2 passed" for "AMX QK does not digress from the AVX dotter" and "AMX PV stays inside the AVX weight-rounding envelope" (plus the child's "VNNI QK identity" case) and must contain no line with "AMX unavailable on this host". State why: "Catch2 passed" alone is printed for the early-return path too [t/testlog.cpp@2880c3aa9b:21-25]. (3) Keep the TRON_AMX_DISABLE=1 control run optional. If used, set the variable in the environment before the process starts, because available() reads it once at the first call and caches the result [src/tron/kernels/amx_attn.cpp@2880c3aa9b:76-80]. Suggested row text after "evidence that the kernel ran": "Evidence is the Slice run log of t_amx_numerics from delphi-3bda with Catch2 passed lines for the AMX precision cases and no 'AMX unavailable on this host' line [S13 AMX availability check, S19 Case execution records]; a Catch2 passed line alone does not prove execution, because the availability early return also ends with zero failed assertions." Also fix S13: it labels the early return "Child:" [design-r3.txt:577], but the same early returns exist on main [t/t_amx_numerics.cpp@2880c3aa9b:145-147, 189-191], so the row applies to the parent as well.

### H01 [round-3 NEW finding, severity MINOR] Local gate list omits make lint-notes, which the required Lint job runs
- Where in the round-3 design: 8 Commands and execution hosts, design-r3.txt:477; 6 Copies and hardware preparation, design-r3.txt:362-363
- Relates to earlier id(s): G26, N28, C06
- Kind: omission | found by lenses: completeness critic
- Claim: The design's local check list is make format, make lint, make build-test, make test-host. The required Lint job runs bin/ci/run-lint-checks.sh, which builds the Nix check lint-toolchain, and that check ends with make lint-notes. lint-notes is a separate Make target and is not a dependency of lint. It fails on any 'See Note [X]' whose definition is missing or lacks the '~~~' underline. The parent edits Note [Zero-Initialized V Slots], which eight comments reference (kv_cache.hpp:1056, 1886, 1905, 2197, 2298, 2337, 2378; t/t_llama_unit.cpp:2650), and it relocates layout documentation into v_vnni.hpp with cross-references from four other files. A malformed or renamed Note passes make lint locally and fails the required check. The checker also needs the .#lint dev shell or GHC; the plain dev shell prints an error.
- The round-3 design text said: "Run targeted suites, then make format, make lint, make build-test, and make test-host in the normal 16-lane AMX-off configuration."
- Evidence given in round-3: GNUmakefile@2880c3aa9b:675-683 (lint-notes target; 'ERROR: lint-notes requires the compiled checker or GHC and Cabal. Preferred: enter nix develop .#lint'), :693-731 (lint recipe: diff --check, cassert grep, clang-format, cargo fmt, lint-ci-pins, lint-agent-guidance; no lint-notes). flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:1571 (lintRuntimeDeps = lintToolchainDeps ++ [ lintNotes ]) and the lint check body, whose last commands are bin/ci/check-lock-freshness.sh then 'make lint-notes'. bin/ci/run-lint-checks.sh@2880c3aa9b:7 (default check_ref .#checks.x86_64-linux.lint-toolchain). .github/workflows/gcp-nix.yml@2880c3aa9b:43-56 (Lint job runs run-lint-checks.sh). bin/lint-notes@2880c3aa9b:15-31 (definition = 'Note [Name]' plus '~~~' line; reference = 'See Note [Name]'). git grep 'Zero-Initialized V Slots' at 2880c3aa9b over h src t: 9 lines, 1 definition (kv_cache.hpp:2055) and 8 references.
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): At design-r3.txt:477 replace the sentence with: 'Run targeted suites, then make format, make lint, make lint-notes (inside nix develop .#lint --accept-flake-config, which supplies the checker), make build-test, and make test-host in the normal 16-lane AMX-off configuration. The required Lint job runs nix build .#checks.x86_64-linux.lint-toolchain, which includes lint-notes; make lint alone does not.' After design-r3.txt:363 add: 'Write the relocated documentation as a repository Note (a Note [Name] heading underlined with ~~~) and each cross-reference as See Note [Name]. Keep the name Note [Zero-Initialized V Slots]; eight comments reference it.'
- LEAD NOTE (final grade minor): Holds 3 of 3. Correction: drop the sentence that the plain dev shell prints an error; the default shell inherits the ingest GHC and Cabal and runs the slower source checker, while nix develop .#lint supplies the compiled one. The parent edits Note [Zero-Initialized V Slots], which eight comments reference, and relocates layout documentation with cross-references, so a malformed Note passes make lint locally and fails the required Lint check.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (source lens): Three corrections and one added mitigation. (1) Drop the sentence "The checker also needs the .#lint dev shell or GHC; the plain dev shell prints an error." The plain `nix develop` shell inherits the ingest Haskell shell (flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:544 `inputsFrom = [ targetIngestFlake.devShells.default ]`, cabal tool at :329), and README.md@2880c3aa9b:237-247 documents `nix develop` as providing the pinned GHC and Cabal for `make lint-notes`. In that shell the recipe takes the `bin/lint-notes` (Cabal script) branch (GNUmakefile@2880c3aa9b:678-679); the error text (:681-683) prints only when neither `tron-lint-notes` nor GHC+Cabal is on PATH, i.e. outside both shells. The `.#lint` shell (flake.nix:1618-1625, packages = lintRuntimeDeps ++ [ lefthookDev ]) adds the precompiled `tron-lint-notes`. Reword the recommendation's parenthetical to: "(the .#lint shell supplies the precompiled checker; the default shell runs the bin/lint-notes script through its GHC and Cabal)". (2) Fix the citation "GNUmakefile@2880c3aa9b:675-683" to ":675-685" (the recipe's `fi` is at 685). (3) Replace "cross-references from four other files" with "cross-references at four sites in three files (amx_attn_iface.hpp, self_attention.hpp, model.hpp twice)" per design-r3.txt:363. Added mitigation, to narrow "passes make lint locally and fails the required check": lefthook.yml@2880c3aa9b:214-238 defines a pre-commit `lint-notes` command with globs `h/**/*.{hpp,h,i}`, `src/**/*.cpp`, `t/**/*.cpp` that runs `make lint-notes`, and the default dev shell registers that hook when CI is unset (flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:1611-1616 installNixHooks, appended to the default shell hook at :1851). So a commit made from the dev shell with hooks active does run lint-notes; the gap is the design's explicit checklist, commits made with hooks bypassed, and the hook's silent "Skipping: lint-notes checker or GHC/Cabal not found" path outside Nix (lefthook.yml:235). Also make the second recommendation conditional: the design does not require the relocated v_vnni.hpp documentation to be a Note, so say "If the relocated documentation is written as a Note, give the heading the `~~~` underline (bin/lint-notes@2880c3aa9b:132-139) and write each cross-reference as `See Note [Name]`; keep the name Note [Zero-Initialized V Slots] unchanged, since eight comments reference it." Mechanism nit for the finding text: a heading without `~~~` is not parsed as a definition, so the checker reports the eight references as "Reference to undefined" (bin/lint-notes:283-284) rather than as a malformed definition (:115-116 covers only a missing `]` or a name under 5 characters); the check fails either way.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (design-text lens): Narrow two parts and keep the rest. (1) Drop the sentence "The checker also needs the .#lint dev shell or GHC; the plain dev shell prints an error." The default shell built by mkTronDevShell includes the ingest shell and sets GHC="ghc" and CABAL_INSTALL="cabal" [flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:1742-1743, 1868-1870; ghc-on-PATH comment at 633]. With ghc and cabal present, make lint-notes takes the bin/lint-notes fallback branch instead of printing the error [GNUmakefile@2880c3aa9b:678-679]; the error text prints only when neither the compiled checker nor GHC+Cabal is found [GNUmakefile@2880c3aa9b:680-684]. Replace with: "In the default shell, make lint-notes compiles bin/lint-notes with the ingest GHC and Cabal; nix develop .#lint supplies the prebuilt tron-lint-notes [flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:1521-1533, 1571, 1617-1620]." (2) Do not ask the design to mandate that the relocated v_vnni.hpp documentation be written as a repository Note; no repository rule requires that form, and a plain comment pointing at the header passes lint-notes. Replace the second recommendation with: "After design-r3.txt:363 add: If the relocated documentation becomes a Note, underline its heading with ~~~ and write each cross-reference as See Note [Name]; keep the name Note [Zero-Initialized V Slots] unchanged, since eight comments reference it [bin/lint-notes@2880c3aa9b:15-31; git grep at 2880c3aa9b]." (3) Keep the first recommendation for design-r3.txt:477 as written, and add that the design's list mirrors README.ci.md's local-reproduction block [README.ci.md@2880c3aa9b:655-664], so the design should also name the CI-equivalent command nix build .#checks.x86_64-linux.lint-toolchain -L --no-update-lock-file [README.ci.md@2880c3aa9b:169-175] as the authoritative local reproduction of the Lint job. (4) Optionally note the mitigation: the default dev shell installs lefthook hooks [flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:1851] and the lint-notes hook runs on commits touching h/**/*.hpp, src/**/*.cpp and t/**/*.cpp [lefthook.yml@2880c3aa9b:214-238]; this does not remove the omission because the design asks implementers to report skipped checks by name from its own list [design-r3.txt:477] and never mentions the hook.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (intent lens): Narrow and fix four points. (1) "cross-references from four other files" should read "three files at four sites": amx_attn_iface.hpp:224-227, self_attention.hpp:31-35, model.hpp:209-213 and model.hpp:2816-2820 [design-r3.txt:363]. (2) Drop "the plain dev shell prints an error". The default dev shell inherits the ingest Haskell shell [flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:1708 inputsFrom targetIngestShell; :1742-1743 GHC = "ghc", CABAL_INSTALL = "cabal"], so make lint-notes takes the GHC-and-Cabal branch and runs the source checker bin/lint-notes [GNUmakefile@2880c3aa9b:678-679]; the compiled tron-lint-notes is on PATH only in the .#lint shell [flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:1571, 1617-1622] and inside the lefthook wrapper [flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:1598-1599]. Reword to: "the plain dev shell runs the slower source checker through Cabal; nix develop .#lint --accept-flake-config supplies the compiled checker; the exact CI reproduction is nix build .#checks.x86_64-linux.lint-toolchain -L --no-update-lock-file [README.ci.md@2880c3aa9b:171-175; lefthook.yml@2880c3aa9b:6-7]". (3) Drop "renamed Note": the design keeps the name [design-r3.txt:362]. State the risk as a malformed definition (the ~~~ rule must start at the "Note [" column on the next line [bin/lint-notes@2880c3aa9b:132-139]) or a dangling "See Note [...]" introduced by the relocation at design-r3.txt:363 or by the child port of Note [K VNNI storage] and Note [Shared block save]. (4) Add context: README.ci.md's own "Reproducing CI Locally" block lists make lint without lint-notes [README.ci.md@2880c3aa9b:653-664], so the design followed the documented list; cite README.ci.md:169-175 when adding the command. Mention the lefthook pre-commit hook [lefthook.yml@2880c3aa9b:214-238] as a partial safeguard that skips silently when the checker is absent [:229-236]. Also: the lint-notes target spans GNUmakefile:675-685, not 675-683. Make the second recommended sentence conditional: "If the relocated documentation is written as a repository Note (a Note [Name] heading underlined with ~~~ on the next line), write each cross-reference as See Note [Name]; keep the name Note [Zero-Initialized V Slots], which eight comments reference." The first recommended sentence for design-r3.txt:477 stands.

### H02 [round-3 NEW finding, severity MINOR] Child port names no Haskell format gate and not ingest/AGENTS.md
- Where in the round-3 design: 7 Then adapt PR #4424 as a stacked child, design-r3.txt:390; 7 Worker-index request, design-r3.txt:424
- Relates to earlier id(s): N33, N35, G23, F29
- Kind: omission | found by lenses: completeness critic
- Claim: The child edits ingest/src/TronCpp.hs and ingest/test/LoopyTronSpec.hs. The repository has a scoped guide for that directory, ingest/AGENTS.md. It requires bin/ingest-cabal build, bin/ingest-cabal test, make format-haskell, and running inside nix develop. The required Lint check runs fourmolu --mode check over every .hs file under ingest/src and ingest/test, with make format-haskell as the fix command. The design names .github/AGENTS.md and README.ci.md for the workflow flag and bin/ingest-cabal test for the spec update. It names neither ingest/AGENTS.md nor make format-haskell. An implementer who follows the page runs the C++ formatter and the Haskell tests, and finds the Fourmolu failure only in CI.
- The round-3 design text said: "Read .github/AGENTS.md and README.ci.md before porting the packed-K workflow flag and its README paragraph together."
- Evidence given in round-3: ingest/AGENTS.md@2880c3aa9b:24-33 ('Run from the repository root inside nix develop'; 'Haskell: use bin/ingest-cabal build, bin/ingest-cabal test, and make format-haskell'). git ls-tree at 2880c3aa9b lists four scoped guides: AGENTS.md, .github/AGENTS.md, ingest/AGENTS.md, t/AGENTS.md. flake.nix@2880c3aa9b lint check body: 'find ingest/src ingest/test -type f -name *.hs' then lint-check.sh 'Haskell formatting (Fourmolu)' with fix 'make format-haskell' and 'fourmolu ... --mode check'. GNUmakefile@2880c3aa9b:762-763 (format-haskell target). design-r3.txt:424 says only 'Run bin/ingest-cabal test.'
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): At design-r3.txt:390 add 'Read ingest/AGENTS.md before editing the generator or its spec.' At design-r3.txt:424 replace 'Run bin/ingest-cabal test.' with 'Run make format-haskell, then bin/ingest-cabal test, inside nix develop. The required Lint check runs fourmolu --mode check over ingest/src and ingest/test.'
- LEAD NOTE (final grade minor): Holds 3 of 3, child scope only (the parent touches no .hs file). Corrections: :476 already says nix develop; :477 "Follow the current scoped guides" routes to ingest/AGENTS.md through the root guide's table, so the gap is selective naming (.github/AGENTS.md is named, ingest/AGENTS.md is not) and a missing make format-haskell command. The child head's Lint job fails at clang-format and stops, so the Fourmolu check has not yet run on the child.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (source lens): Narrow the claim: remove 'running inside nix develop' from the list of things the design does not name, because design-r3.txt:476 says 'Use nix develop --accept-flake-config and the pinned toolchain'. Add that design-r3.txt:477 'Follow the current scoped guides' gives an indirect route to ingest/AGENTS.md via AGENTS.md@2880c3aa9b:92-96, so the omission is that the child section (design-r3.txt:390) names .github/AGENTS.md explicitly but not ingest/AGENTS.md, the guide for the two Haskell files the port edits. Fix the evidence: the fix command as CI prints it is 'nix develop .#lint --accept-flake-config -c make format-haskell' [flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:2221; lint-check.sh:14,17], and the check also covers ingest/Main.hs [flake.nix:2223]. Add the make-target facts: make format (GNUmakefile@2880c3aa9b:819-823) runs clang-format, format-python, and format-shell only; make lint (693-696) does not run Fourmolu. Add the live evidence: the child PR's Lint run 35227590189 on 30c4ac82cb failed at clang-format (k_vnni.hpp:106) and stopped before the Fourmolu check, so the lint derivation reports one failing check per CI round. Reword the recommendation: at design-r3.txt:390 add 'Read ingest/AGENTS.md (the scoped guide for the generator directory) before editing ingest/src/TronCpp.hs or ingest/test/LoopyTronSpec.hs [AGENTS.md:92-96].' At design-r3.txt:424 replace 'Run bin/ingest-cabal test.' with 'Run make format-haskell (the Lint check's fix command), then bin/ingest-cabal test. The required Lint check runs fourmolu --mode check over ingest/Main.hs and every .hs file under ingest/src and ingest/test; make format and make lint do not run it.' Drop G23 and N35 from relates_to; keep N33 and F29.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (design-text lens): Narrow the claim and fix three citations. Claim: replace "It names neither ingest/AGENTS.md nor make format-haskell" with "It names .github/AGENTS.md and README.ci.md for the child port [design-r3.txt:390] but not ingest/AGENTS.md, and its explicit target list make format, make lint [design-r3.txt:477] contains no Haskell formatter. The generic sentence 'Follow the current scoped guides' [design-r3.txt:477] does route to ingest/AGENTS.md through the root guide's table [AGENTS.md@2880c3aa9b:92-98], so the gap is selective naming, not a total omission." Add the supporting fact that the child head's Lint job fails at the C++ clang-format check and stops there, so the Haskell check has not yet run on the child [check-run 105223012912 for 30c4ac82cb; bin/ci/lint-check.sh@2880c3aa9b:11-18]. Citations: ingest/AGENTS.md@2880c3aa9b:25-28 (not 24-33); flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:2217-2224, and the file set is ingest/Main.hs plus ingest/src and ingest/test (add Main.hs); GNUmakefile@2880c3aa9b:819-823 (`format` has no Haskell step) and :693-731 (`lint` has no fourmolu step) are the load-bearing lines, more than :762-763. Recommendation: keep the two insertions, but drop "inside nix develop" from the line-424 text because line 476 already requires nix develop. Suggested text for design-r3.txt:424: "Run make format-haskell, then bin/ingest-cabal test. The required Lint check runs fourmolu --mode check over ingest/Main.hs and every .hs file under ingest/src and ingest/test; make format and make lint do not cover Haskell." Optional one-line alternative at design-r3.txt:477: change "make format" to "the language-specific format targets (make format for C++; make format-haskell for the child's Haskell edits)". Note: the relates_to id "G23" is not a round-1 or round-2 id in r1-findings.md or r2-findings.md; N33 (worker-index step) and F29 (generator file not named) are the right ancestors, N35 is unrelated (K row expression).
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (intent lens): Keep the finding as a new minor completeness item in child scope. Apply these corrections: (1) Add "(child scope)" to the claim: the parent touches no .hs file [facts-r3.md:40-42], so only the PR 4424 port is affected. (2) Cite ingest/AGENTS.md@2880c3aa9b:25-28 (not 24-33) for the two quoted sentences, and GNUmakefile@2880c3aa9b:762-764 (not 762-763) for the format-haskell recipe. (3) Add the mechanism sentence: "make lint, which the design names at design-r3.txt:477, does not run fourmolu [GNUmakefile@2880c3aa9b:693-731]; only the CI Lint check does [flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:2217-2224], and lint-check.sh stops at the first failing check [bin/ci/lint-check.sh:18]." (4) Acknowledge the design's indirect pointer: design-r3.txt:477 "Follow the current scoped guides" plus AGENTS.md@2880c3aa9b:92-98 does lead to ingest/AGENTS.md; the gap is the missing explicit name and command, not a contradiction. (5) Replace "finds the Fourmolu failure only in CI" with "would find any Fourmolu failure only in CI"; whether the child's .hs text currently passes fourmolu is Insufficient data, because PR 4424's live Lint job (run 35227590189, head 30c4ac82cb) fails earlier on a clang-format violation at h/tron/kernels/k_vnni.hpp:106 and never reaches the Haskell check. Use that job as evidence that format-only Lint failures on this branch are discovered in CI, not as proof of a Fourmolu failure. (6) Recommendation wording is valid: fourmolu is on PATH in the default nix develop shell (the Tron dev shell inherits the ingest shell's haskell.nix tools, which include fourmolu [flake.nix@2880c3aa9b:314-334, 1868-1873]) and in the .#lint shell that CI's fix hint names [flake.nix:2221, 2336]. Optionally add "and ingest/Main.hs" to the file set the Lint check covers [flake.nix:2223]. (7) State that this is a new item, not a residual of N33, N35 or F29, whose corrected recommendations asked for the ingest tests and the generator file name but not the format gate [r2-findings.md:462-464; r1-findings.md:303].

### G05 [round-3 NEW finding, severity NOTE] Issue draft keeps the reviewer's name through 'preserve existing links' and gives the role sentence no link
- Where in the round-3 design: 2. Proposed issue #4525 text (design-r3.txt:107, :118); 9. rows N42 (:528) and F19 F20 F21 (:547)

Applied as written, the edit leaves the name in the issue twice and drops the two review links from the replacement sentence. jhan's rule for issues that agents draft is roles plus review links. The round-2 N42 recommendation and the F21 residual asked for 'the reviewing maintainer's' in place of 'Ben's' with the links unchanged; the round-3 block does not say that, and the remaining cells of rows 528 and 547 do not record the gap. The proposal block sits inside a details element collapsed by default.

Preserve existing reviewer links and the issue's remaining child-adaptation checklist. [...] Follow the reviewing maintainer's request for typed cache arguments."
- Evidence given in round-3: design-r3.txt:107 and :118; the HTML paragraph at :118 contains no <a> element (design-new-tensor-type.html, details id="issue-proposal", no open attribute at HTML line 98). Live issue body (gh issue view 4525 -R positron-ai/tron, 2026-09-22, updatedAt 2026-09-22T00:11:35Z) and evidence/github-review.json key issue.body: 'This follows Ben's [tensor-interface review](https://github.com/positron-ai/tron/pull/4424#pullrequestreview-5270587330) and [API sketch](https://github.com/positron-ai/tron/pull/4424#issuecomment-5765866077)' and PR-order item 3 'Ben's [save_k worker-interface comment](https://github.com/positron-ai/tron/pull/4424#discussion_r4065141577) remains a separate concern there.' facts-r3.md:46 (jhan's rule: no names in issues Claude drafts; body still names Ben twice). r2-findings.md N42 RECOMMENDATION ('the reviewing maintainer's' in place of 'Ben's' with links unchanged); r2-findings.md F21 STILL NEEDED. Row texts design-r3.txt:528 and :547. checker#14 (merged into the Scope-bullets finding) raises the same missing-link point.
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): Replace the last sentence of design-r3.txt:107 with: 'Rewrite the two sentences that name the reviewer so they use the role and keep their links: "This follows the reviewing maintainer's [tensor-interface review](https://github.com/positron-ai/tron/pull/4424#pullrequestreview-5270587330) and [API sketch](https://github.com/positron-ai/tron/pull/4424#issuecomment-5765866077)." - LEAD NOTE (final grade note): Holds 3 of 3; the intent verifier lowered it to note and the lead agrees: the name replacement in the live issue is F21, which round 2 left to jhan, and the design's deferral of the public edit is acceptable. What remains is a wording gap inside the new block: the role sentence at :118 carries no link, and the block does not say what to do with the two live sentences that carry the three review links. Do not attribute the "reviewing maintainer" phrase to the F21 residual. (b) Outcome claim: replace "leaves the name in the issue twice and drops the two review links from the replacement sentence" with: "Under the block's own instruction to preserve the links, the intro sentence and PR-order item 3 stay verbatim, so the name stays twice. If the assignee instead treats the intro sentence as one of the replaced problem paragraphs, the name stays once (item 3) and the two review links are lost. Either outcome misses the N42 recommendation." (c) Kind: "omission (N42 partly)" fits better than "contradiction"; the block does not contradict itself, it omits the name-replacement instruction. (d) Drop the "add the open attribute" recommendation or mark it optional: the section-9 links target the details element's own id, so the summary line is visible after navigation; collapsed-by-default is not a defect. (e) The block's phrase "the issue's remaining child-adaptation checklist" [design-r3.txt:107, :120] names something the live body does not contain; the body has a 3-item "PR order" list and no checklist. The finding's recommendation already says "Keep the PR order list", which is the right name; state the slip explicitly so codex renames it. (f) Context worth one clause: the design's own Words table now names the reviewer ("Ben, the Tron maintainer who reviewed PR #4424") [design-r3.txt:45], and section 2 links all three comments [design-r3-links.tsv:5-7]. The no-names rule covers GitHub issues, not this local page, so that is not a violation, but the proposed issue text should use one role term consistently; "the reviewing maintainer" is fine. (g) Saved-vs-live: the saved JSON predates jhan's 00:10-00:11 UTC edit (first sentence ends "before #4424" and an extra final sentence exists in the saved copy); both "Ben's" sentences and all three links are identical in both, so the finding's evidence stands, but cite the live body as primary. (h) Timing "before the parent PR opens, for example with gh issue edit 4525 --body-file" is a proposal from the F19 residual, not a stated requirement [r2-findings.md F19 re-grader (fairness)]; keep it as an example.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (design-text lens): Narrow the finding in four places.

1. Do not grade N42 as unresolved. The round-2 verifier corrections narrowed N42 to "name the assignee" and said "No duplicated paste block is required" [batches/B16.md:11-12]. The design names the assignee twice [design-r3.txt:88, :107]. Row 528 needs no change. The name-replacement gap belongs to F21 and to the internal consistency of the new block.

2. Fix the attribution of the wording. The F21 residual asked for "the reviewer's", not "the reviewing maintainer's" [r2-findings.md:946]. "The reviewing maintainer's" came from N42's original recommendation [batches/B16.md:9] and from one round-1 verifier [r1-findings.md:243]. Either role wording satisfies the rule.

3. Reword "drops the two review links from the replacement sentence". The precise defect is: the new role sentence carries no link [design-new-tensor-type.html:103], and the draft neither replaces nor explicitly preserves the live paragraph "This follows Ben's ..." [design-r3.txt:107 versus the live body]. Keeping that paragraph keeps the name. Dropping it loses the links. The block gives no third option.

4. Drop the collapsed-details point or mark it note. Browsers scroll to the summary line when the fragment targets the details element; adding open is optional polish.

Recommendation, corrected: append one sentence to design-r3.txt:107: "Rewrite the two sentences that name the reviewer so they use the role and keep their links: paragraph 2 becomes 'This follows the reviewing maintainer's [tensor-interface review](https://github.com/positron-ai/tron/pull/4424#pullrequestreview-5270587330) and [API sketch](https://github.com/positron-ai/tron/pull/4424#issuecomment-5765866077).' PR-order item 3 becomes 'The reviewing maintainer's [save_k worker-interface comment](https://github.com/positron-ai/tron/pull/4424#discussion_r4065141577) remains a separate concern there.'" If codex prefers to keep F21 as jhan's decision, write in row 547's remaining cell: "The name replacement (F21) is not in the prepared text; it stays jhan's decision." One of the two is required; both together are not.

Severity if the correction is applied: minor for the block's internal inconsistency. The F21 part alone is note, matching the round-1 verifier severity and the round-2 accepted deferral.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (intent lens): Narrow the finding to: kind = omission (under-specification), severity = note, relates_to F21 N42 (drop F19).

Claim, corrected: The new issue-text block [design-r3.txt:107-120] gives the role sentence "Follow the reviewing maintainer's request for typed cache arguments." [:118] without a link to that request (review 5270587330 or comment 5765866077). It tells the assignee to "Preserve existing reviewer links" [:107] but does not say what to do with the two live sentences that carry those links and name the reviewer (issue body line 3 and PR-order item 3, gh issue view 4525, updatedAt 2026-09-22T00:11:35Z). Applied literally, the issue keeps both names. That outcome is F21, which round 2 left to jhan's decision [r2-findings.md:946], so the design's deferral [design-r3.txt:547] is acceptable. The block also refers to "the issue's remaining child-adaptation checklist" [:107, :120]; the live body has no checklist, only the "PR order" list.

Recommendation, corrected (all optional, low risk if skipped): (1) At design-r3.txt:118 add the two links inline: "Follow the reviewing maintainer's request for typed cache arguments ([tensor-interface review](https://github.com/positron-ai/tron/pull/4424#pullrequestreview-5270587330), [API sketch](https://github.com/positron-ai/tron/pull/4424#issuecomment-5765866077))." (2) At design-r3.txt:107 add one clause: "Keep the sentence 'This follows ...' and PR-order item 3 as they are, or replace the name with 'the reviewing maintainer's' and keep their links unchanged; the assignee decides (round-1 F21)." ### G14 [round-3 NEW finding, severity NOTE] 8-lane class-completeness rule serves only dead fake code; moving the fakes under TRON_AMX_DISPATCH removes it
- Where in the round-3 design: 5. Build and include boundaries (design-r3.txt:281); 7. step 1 (:372); child paragraph (:414); 8. build table 8-lane row (:454)
- Relates to earlier id(s): N01 N61
- Kind: scope | found by lenses: simplicity
- Claim: The rule that the packed V class definitions (and later the K definitions) must stay complete in the 8-lane build has exactly one client: the fake kernel definitions in t/t_amx_dispatch_dtype.cpp. Those fakes are dead code whenever TRON_AMX_DISPATCH is off, and TRON_AMX_DISPATCH cannot be on at 8 lanes. The parent edits that test file anyway (step 6). Moving the fake block under the file's existing #ifdef TRON_AMX_DISPATCH removes the width-neutral class rule, the chunk_size-neutral constraint wording, the parallel child rule, and the t_amx_dispatch_dtype comparison in the 8-lane row. The design does not weigh this smaller option.
- The round-3 design text said: "Keep packed V class definitions and typed kernel declarations available at both chunk widths. The unconditional fake AMX definitions require complete by-value parameter types. This does not require 8-lane implementations of packed-V SIMD operations."
- Evidence given in round-3: t/t_amx_dispatch_dtype.cpp@2880c3aa9b:166-190 (namespace tron::amx_attn_h128g4, qk_rowmajor_128x4 at :180, weights_times_v_128x4 at :185) and the anonymous-namespace probe at :47-164; the file's own guard #ifdef TRON_AMX_DISPATCH starts only at :192. t/CMakeLists.txt@2880c3aa9b:596 (test registered unconditionally). With TRON_AMX_DISPATCH off nothing references amx_attn_h128g4: h/tron/models/self_attention.hpp@2880c3aa9b:259-267, :281-290, :1429-1464 (all call sites inside #ifdef TRON_AMX_DISPATCH); src/tron/CMakeLists.txt@2880c3aa9b:219-223 (amx_attn.cpp compiled only under if(TRON_AMX_DISPATCH)). self_attention.hpp@2880c3aa9b:30-36 (#error at 8 lanes). t/t_amx_numerics.cpp@2880c3aa9b:22-266 (already wrapped in #ifdef TRON_AMX_DISPATCH). A function declaration may name an incomplete class type as a by-value parameter, so the typed declarations in amx_attn_iface.hpp compile at 8 lanes with forward declarations alone. design-r3.txt:377 (step 6 edits t_amx_dispatch_dtype.cpp). Round-2 N01 and N61 established that only class completeness is needed at 8 lanes; neither considered removing that need.
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): Replace design-r3.txt:281 with: 'Move the fake AMX kernel definitions and their probe (t/t_amx_dispatch_dtype.cpp:47-190) under the file's existing #ifdef TRON_AMX_DISPATCH (:192). They are dead code when the option is off, and the option is a compile error at 8 lanes. The packed V class definitions and operation bodies then compile only under TRON_CHUNK_SIZE == 16; the 8-lane build sees only kv_cache_fwd.hpp's forward declarations, which suffice for the by-value kernel declarations.' In step 1 (:372) delete 'Keep class definitions available in both widths.' In the child paragraph (:414) delete 'Keep K class definitions complete in both vector widths for unconditional fake definitions.' In the 8-lane row (:454) replace 'Compare baseline/head builds of tron and t_amx_dispatch_dtype' with 'Compare baseline/head builds of tron; t_amx_dispatch_dtype has no unguarded AMX symbols after the move.' If the design prefers to keep the fakes unconditional, state why in one sentence.
- LEAD NOTE (final grade note): Holds 3 of 3 (one at note); lowered to note by the lead. The both-widths rule is round-2 N01's own corrected recommendation, which the design followed. G14 offers a simplification neither round considered: guard the fakes under TRON_AMX_DISPATCH and the rule disappears. Cost of the move: every AMX-off configuration, including the required Nix lane, stops compiling any kernel definition against the typed declarations. The design may adopt or decline this in one sentence.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (source lens): Four narrowings, none changing the verdict.

1. Fairness to the design: the design followed the reviewers' own instruction. Round-2 N01's corrected recommendation ends with "The typed kernel declarations and the fake definitions stay unconditional" [r2-findings.md, N01 RECOMMENDATION], and section 9 records that adoption [design-r3.txt:512]. Reword the claim so G14 is a new option not offered before, not a compliance failure: "Round-2 N01 told the design to keep the fakes unconditional; this finding proposes the alternative that N01 did not consider."

2. Citation completeness: the finding lists self_attention.hpp:259-267, :281-290, :1429-1464 as "all call sites", but those blocks hold only the packed-Q slot, the scratch vector, and the region bracket. The kernel calls themselves are in :1571-1701 (pack :1586, qk :1654, pv :1680); further guarded blocks are :1531-1538 and :1748-1771. Add them; the conclusion (every use is guarded) is unchanged. Also cite src/tron/CMakeLists.txt:219-224 (the endif is at :224).

3. State one consequence in the recommendation: after the move, the fakes compile only in the 16-lane AMX-on configuration [design-r3.txt:453], so the required Nix combination (16 lanes, AMX off, :452) no longer compiles them at all. That is not a functional loss, because in that configuration the fakes are dead code and a signature mismatch is only observable with AMX on (it would make the linker pull the real amx_attn.o). Say so in one sentence so an implementer does not read the change as lost coverage.

4. Keep design-r3.txt:284 as is. The recommendation's replacement text for :281 must not remove "amx_attn_iface.hpp includes kv_cache_fwd.hpp", because self_attention.hpp:21 includes the interface header in every build and the typed declarations still need the forward declarations at 8 lanes. The finding's replacement text is compatible with :284 but should say explicitly that :284 stays.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (design-text lens): Three narrowings. (a) Say that the design followed round-2 guidance: the N01 verifier corrections (source lens, r2-findings.md:114; intent lens) explicitly told codex to "keep typed kernel declarations and fakes unconditional", and the design's :281 sentence is the result (design-wdiff.txt:899-901). G14 therefore reverses a round-2 recommendation and must state that, as the F39 precedent did (r2-findings.md:328). (b) Drop the claim that the move removes "the chunk_size-neutral constraint wording": the constraint "columns divisible by chunk_size" [design-r3.txt:283] mirrors the existing kv_block array declaration `bf16s v[page_size / 2][head_size / chunk_size * 2]` (kv_cache.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2198) and stays natural at 16 lanes, where chunk_size == 16; the move removes only the NEED for width-neutral classes, not that sentence. (c) Add the one fact that favors the status quo, and its limit: with the fakes unconditional, the required Nix lane (16 lanes, AMX off) compiles the fake definitions, but that checks nothing extra, because the 16-lane kv_cache.hpp already instantiates the same classes and a definition whose signature drifts from the declaration is merely a new overload until an AMX-on link needs it. Recommendation text otherwise stands; keep :284 ("amx_attn_iface.hpp includes kv_cache_fwd.hpp") as is, and apply the same guard move to the child's copy of the file (30c4ac82cb:170-205 under its :207 guard) when :414 is deleted. Severity minor; note is acceptable if the panel weighs the design's compliance with round-2 guidance more heavily.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (intent lens): Narrow the finding to an option the design may adopt or decline in one sentence, and fix three parts of the recommendation. (a) State that design-r3.txt:281 follows round-2 N01's recommendation verbatim, so the both-widths rule is the review's own earlier advice, not a codex error; the guard move is a further simplification neither round considered. (b) Name the cost of the move, which the finding omits: every AMX-off configuration, including the required Nix lane (16 lanes, AMX off, design-r3.txt:452), today compiles the fake definitions against the typed declarations in amx_attn_iface.hpp; after the move only the manual AMX-on build (design-r3.txt:453) compiles any kernel definition. That lost check is small, since kv_cache.hpp carries the full class definitions into every translation unit and self_attention.hpp@2880c3aa9b:21 includes the interface header unconditionally, but the design should weigh it. (c) Do not replace the 8-lane row's comparison (design-r3.txt:454) with 't_amx_dispatch_dtype has no unguarded AMX symbols after the move'. Building t_amx_dispatch_dtype at 8 lanes still compiles model.hpp, kv_cache.hpp and amx_attn_iface.hpp (t/t_amx_dispatch_dtype.cpp@2880c3aa9b:39-41), so the baseline/head comparison keeps its value; only the 'unconditional fake definitions' rationale would go. (d) If the move is adopted, the probe (:47-164) must move with the fakes (:166-190), since the fakes reference the probe global at :57; the finding's recommendation already covers this, keep it. Suggested design change, if any: add one sentence after design-r3.txt:281 that either adopts the guard move (with the edits to :372, :414 and :454) or says why the fakes stay unconditional (for example, to keep the fake-signature compile check in the AMX-off lanes).

### G20 [round-3 NEW finding, severity NOTE] Line 319 restricts model.hpp comment edits to comments that name the moved operation; line 363 lists model.hpp:2816-2820, which names none
- Where in the round-3 design: 6. V padding and write ordering (design-r3.txt:319); Copies and hardware preparation (:363); 7. step 3 (:374)
- Relates to earlier id(s): N27 C02
- Kind: factual error | found by lenses: disposition-checker
- Claim: design-r3.txt:319 restricts the model.hpp comment edits to 'the partner-scheduling comments that name the moved V operation', but :363 asks to cross-reference the relocated layout documentation from 'model.hpp:209-213,2816-2820'. Only the first of those two comments names the operation: model.hpp:209-213 says 'because page.set_v interleaves them in the same SIMD values'; model.hpp:2816-2820 says 'we're interleaving the even-offset and odd-offset tokens of any given page in the same SIMD values' and names no function. An implementer following :319 literally skips the 2816-2820 edit that :363 requires. 'The moved V operation' is also not a defined phrase: :258 says append_v_row moves the set_v bodies, while :268 keeps the name page::set_v.
- The round-3 design text said: "Keep adjust_grains_for_partners, the scheduler's pair-preserving work partition, in model.hpp. Keep token_ix_needed, the page-readiness rule, in common.hpp. Its existing comment is accurate and needs no edit. Update only the partner-scheduling comments that name the moved V operation. [C02]"
- Evidence given in round-3: h/tron/models/model.hpp@2880c3aa9b:209-213 ('because `page.set_v` interleaves them in the same SIMD values'; adjust_grains_for_partners call at 214), :2816-2820 ('Take care: we can't just split the items across threads, because we're interleaving the even-offset and odd-offset tokens of any given page in the same SIMD values'). facts-r3.md:62 records both sites. design-r3.txt:258, :268, :319, :363, :374 ('Update the changed operation's comments'), :522 (section 9 N27 N28 row).
- RECOMMENDATION (what the design was asked to change): Replace the last sentence of :319 with: 'Update only the two partner-scheduling comments in model.hpp (209-213 and 2816-2820 at 2880c3aa9b): add a cross-reference to the v_vnni.hpp layout documentation, and in 209-213 optionally say that page::set_v forwards to append_v_row, which does the pair interleaving.'
- LEAD NOTE (final grade note): Holds 3 of 3 (one at note); lowered to note by the lead and reclassified as ambiguity: every fact is right, and line 363 plus step 6 list both model.hpp sites by number, so the risk is confusion, not a missed edit. The tension comes from round 2: N27 limited the edit to 209-213 and N28 cross-referenced both sites; the design followed each in a different bullet.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (source lens): Reclassify from "factual error" to "ambiguity: inconsistent scope wording". Drop the phrase "restricts the model.hpp comment edits"; say instead that :319's "only ... that name" can be read two ways, and that the section 9 row at design-r3.txt:552 ("Update only changed comments, not common.hpp's accurate readiness comment") shows the intended contrast is model.hpp against common.hpp. Add that model.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2816-2820 does describe the pair interleaving and sits 11 lines above the set_v call at :2831, so the risk is a literal reading of "name", not a missing site. Add the round-2 origin: N27's corrected recommendation limited the edit to model.hpp:209-213, N28's verifier correction included 2816-2820, and the design adopted both. Keep the recommended rewording of the last sentence of :319, pointing at the two sites already listed at :363: "Update the two partner-scheduling comments in model.hpp listed in section 6 (209-213 and 2816-2820 at 2880c3aa9b): cross-reference the v_vnni.hpp layout documentation, and in 209-213 optionally say that page::set_v forwards to append_v_row, which does the pair interleaving." Note that the 209-213 statement "`page.set_v` interleaves them" stays true after the parent because :268 keeps the name page::set_v and forwards it to append_v_row, so the edit there is a cross-reference, not a correction.
- VERIFIER CORRECTION (design-text lens): Change the kind from "factual error" to "internal inconsistency / ambiguity": every source fact in the finding is right, and the defect is that the restrictive test in design-r3.txt:319 ("that name the moved V operation") excludes model.hpp@2880c3aa9b:2816-2820, a site that design-r3.txt:363 lists. Narrow the claim "An implementer following :319 literally skips the 2816-2820 edit" to "An implementer reading :319 alone cannot tell whether 2816-2820 is in scope; :363 and :377 (step 6, 'Update the comment sites listed in section 6') give the explicit list, so the risk is confusion, not a missed edit." Drop or soften "'The moved V operation' is also not a defined phrase": design-r3.txt:258 defines the move (append_v_row moves the packed set_v bodies), and model.hpp@2880c3aa9b:211 names that operation as page.set_v; say "loosely defined" at most. Keep the recommendation; it matches N27's corrected recommendation (optional note that page::set_v forwards to append_v_row) and N28's (cross-reference from both model.hpp sites). A smaller alternative fix: in :319 replace "that name the moved V operation" with "that describe the pair interleaving (model.hpp:209-213 and 2816-2820 at 2880c3aa9b; the second precedes the set_v call at :2831)".

Narrowed claim: design-r3.txt:319 defines the model.hpp comment-edit scope by a property ("comments that name the moved V operation") that, read literally, matches only model.hpp@2880c3aa9b:209-213, while :363 lists two sites (209-213 and 2816-2820); the sentence should point at the list instead of defining the set by a property. The tension originates in round 2: N27's corrected recommendation limited the update to 209-213, and N28's recommendation (3) cross-referenced both sites; the design followed each in a different bullet. Suggested reword of the last sentence of :319: "Update only the two model.hpp partner-scheduling comments listed under Copies and hardware preparation (209-213 and 2816-2820 at 2880c3aa9b): add the v_vnni.hpp layout cross-reference to both, and in 209-213 optionally say that page::set_v forwards to append_v_row, which does the pair interleaving."
